
==================== src\components\admin\activity-admin.tsx ====================
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useState } from "react";
import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
import { formatDateTime } from "@/lib/data";
import { Panel } from "./form";

const areas = [
  { id: "todas", label: "Tudo" },
  { id: "evento", label: "Eventos" },
  { id: "inscricao", label: "InscriÃ§Ãµes" },
  { id: "produto", label: "Produtos" },
  { id: "aviso", label: "Avisos" },
  { id: "pedido", label: "Pedidos" },
  { id: "membro", label: "Membros" },
  { id: "acesso", label: "Acessos" },
  { id: "pagina", label: "PÃ¡ginas" },
] as const;

export function ActivityAdmin() {
  const [area, setArea] = useState<(typeof areas)[number]["id"]>("todas");
  const { data: entries = [], isLoading } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["admin", "activity"],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.from("activity_log").select("id, created_at, actor_email, summary, area").order("created_at", { ascending: false }).limit(200);
      if (error) throw error;
      return data;
    },
  });
  const visible = area === "todas" ? entries : entries.filter((entry) => entry.area === area);

  return <Panel title="HistÃ³rico" subtitle="Quem criou, editou ou excluiu alguma coisa no painel.">
    <div className="flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto border-b border-border p-4">
      {areas.map((item) => <button key={item.id} type="button" onClick={() => setArea(item.id)} className={`h-9 shrink-0 rounded-full px-3 text-xs font-bold ${area === item.id ? "bg-primary text-primary-foreground" : "bg-muted text-muted-foreground"}`}>{item.label}</button>)}
    </div>
    <ul className="divide-y divide-border">
      {isLoading && <li className="p-6"><div className="h-10 animate-pulse rounded-lg bg-muted" /></li>}
      {visible.map((entry) => <li key={entry.id} className="px-5 py-4">
        <p className="text-sm font-bold">{entry.summary}</p>
        <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{entry.actor_email ?? "Conta removida"} Â· {formatDateTime(entry.created_at)}</p>
      </li>)}
      {!isLoading && visible.length === 0 && <li className="p-6 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">Nenhuma alteraÃ§Ã£o registrada ainda.</li>}
    </ul>
  </Panel>;
}


==================== src\components\admin\admin-users.ts ====================
import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";

const errorText: Record<string, string> = {
  invalid_email: "Informe um e-mail vÃ¡lido.",
  weak_password: "A senha precisa ter pelo menos 8 caracteres.",
  email_exists: "JÃ¡ existe uma conta com esse e-mail.",
  protected_user: "Esta conta nÃ£o pode ser alterada por aqui.",
  forbidden: "Somente o responsÃ¡vel pela plataforma pode gerenciar contas.",
  invalid_user: "Esta conta nÃ£o pode ser alterada por aqui.",
};

export function generatePassword() {
  const alphabet = "ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijkmnpqrstuvwxyz23456789";
  const bytes = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint32Array(12));
  return Array.from(bytes, (n) => alphabet[n % alphabet.length]).join("");
}

export async function callAdminUsers(body: Record<string, string>) {
  const { data, error } = await supabase.functions.invoke("admin-users", { body });
  if (!error) return { error: null, code: null, userId: (data?.user_id as string | undefined) ?? null };
  const context = (error as { context?: Response }).context;
  const payload = context ? await context.json().catch(() => null) : null;
  const code = typeof payload?.error === "string" ? payload.error : "failed";
  return { error: errorText[code] ?? "NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel concluir. Tente novamente.", code, userId: null };
}

==================== src\components\admin\announcements-admin.tsx ====================
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { Bell, EyeOff, Pencil, Plus, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import { useState } from "react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
import type { AnnouncementRow } from "@/lib/data";
import { ErrorNote, Field, Panel, Toggle, inputClass } from "./form";

export function AnnouncementsAdmin() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const { data: items = [], isLoading } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["admin", "announcements"],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.from("announcements").select("*").order("sort_order").order("created_at", { ascending: false });
      if (error) throw error;
      return data;
    },
  });
  const [form, setForm] = useState<{ mode: "new" } | { mode: "edit"; item: AnnouncementRow } | null>(null);
  const refresh = () => { queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin"] }); queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["announcements"] }); };
  const remove = async (a: AnnouncementRow) => {
    if (!window.confirm(`Excluir o aviso "${a.title}"?`)) return;
    await supabase.from("announcements").delete().eq("id", a.id);
    refresh();
  };

  return <Panel title="Avisos" subtitle="Recados exibidos na pÃ¡gina inicial." action={<Button onClick={() => setForm({ mode: "new" })}><Plus /> Novo aviso</Button>}>
    {form && <AnnouncementForm key={form.mode === "edit" ? form.item.id : "new"} initial={form.mode === "edit" ? form.item : null} onDone={() => { setForm(null); refresh(); }} onCancel={() => setForm(null)} />}
    <ul className="divide-y divide-border">
      {isLoading && <li className="p-6"><div className="h-12 animate-pulse rounded-lg bg-muted" /></li>}
      {items.map((a) => <li key={a.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 p-4 sm:gap-4 sm:px-5">
        <span className="grid size-10 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full bg-navy-soft text-primary">{a.published ? <Bell size={18} /> : <EyeOff size={18} />}</span>
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1"><p className="truncate font-bold">{a.title}</p><p className="truncate text-xs text-muted-foreground">{a.published ? a.body || "Sem texto" : "Oculto"}</p></div>
        <Button variant="outline" size="icon" onClick={() => setForm({ mode: "edit", item: a })} aria-label={`Editar ${a.title}`}><Pencil size={16} /></Button>
        <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" className="text-destructive" onClick={() => remove(a)} aria-label={`Excluir ${a.title}`}><Trash2 size={16} /></Button>
      </li>)}
      {!isLoading && items.length === 0 && <li className="p-6 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">Nenhum aviso cadastrado.</li>}
    </ul>
  </Panel>;
}

function AnnouncementForm({ initial, onDone, onCancel }: { initial: AnnouncementRow | null; onDone: () => void; onCancel: () => void }) {
  const [title, setTitle] = useState(initial?.title ?? "");
  const [body, setBody] = useState(initial?.body ?? "");
  const [published, setPublished] = useState(initial?.published ?? true);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const save = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault(); setBusy(true); setError("");
    const payload = { title: title.trim(), body: body.trim(), published };
    const { error: dbError } = initial
      ? await supabase.from("announcements").update(payload).eq("id", initial.id)
      : await supabase.from("announcements").insert(payload);
    setBusy(false);
    if (dbError) return setError("NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel salvar o aviso.");
    onDone();
  };
  return <form className="grid gap-4 border-b border-border bg-muted/50 p-5" onSubmit={save}>
    <Field label="TÃ­tulo"><input required value={title} onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)} placeholder="Retirada de carteirinhas" className={inputClass} /></Field>
    <Field label="Texto"><textarea rows={3} value={body} onChange={(e) => setBody(e.target.value)} className={`${inputClass} h-auto py-2`} /></Field>
    <Toggle label="Exibir na pÃ¡gina inicial" checked={published} onChange={setPublished} />
    <ErrorNote text={error} />
    <div className="flex gap-2"><Button type="submit" disabled={busy}>{busy ? "Salvando..." : "Salvar"}</Button><Button type="button" variant="ghost" onClick={onCancel}>Cancelar</Button></div>
  </form>;
}


==================== src\components\admin\board-admin.tsx ====================
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { Crown, KeyRound, Plus, Trash2, UsersRound, Wand2 } from "lucide-react";
import { useState } from "react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
import { formatDateTime } from "@/lib/data";
import { callAdminUsers, generatePassword } from "./admin-users";
import { ErrorNote, Field, Panel, inputClass } from "./form";

export function BoardAdmin({ isOwner }: { isOwner: boolean }) {
  const { data: members = [], refetch, isLoading } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["board"], queryFn: async () => { const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("list_board"); if (error) throw error; return data; } });
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [created, setCreated] = useState<{ email: string; password: string } | null>(null);
  const [resetting, setResetting] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const create = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault(); setBusy(true); setError(""); setCreated(null);
    const failure = await callAdminUsers({ action: "create", email: email.trim(), password });
    setBusy(false);
    if (failure.error) return setError(failure.error);
    setCreated({ email: email.trim().toLowerCase(), password });
    setEmail(""); setPassword("");
    refetch();
  };
  const remove = async (userId: string, memberEmail: string) => {
    if (!window.confirm(`Remover o acesso de ${memberEmail}? A conta serÃ¡ apagada.`)) return;
    const failure = await callAdminUsers({ action: "delete", user_id: userId });
    if (failure.error) return window.alert(failure.error);
    refetch();
  };

  return <Panel title="Acessos ao painel" subtitle={isOwner ? "O acesso de cada membro Ã© criado na ficha, em Membros do DA. Aqui ficam todas as contas do painel." : "Contas com acesso ao painel. Novos acessos sÃ£o criados pelo responsÃ¡vel pela plataforma."}>
    {isOwner && <form onSubmit={create} className="grid gap-4 border-b border-border bg-muted/50 p-5 sm:grid-cols-[1fr_1fr_auto]">
      <Field label="E-mail do novo membro"><input required type="email" value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} placeholder="email@exemplo.com" className={inputClass} /></Field>
      <Field label="Senha inicial" hint="mÃ­nimo de 8 caracteres">
        <div className="flex gap-2"><input required minLength={8} value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} className={`${inputClass} font-mono`} /><Button type="button" variant="outline" size="icon" className="size-11 shrink-0" onClick={() => setPassword(generatePassword())} aria-label="Gerar senha"><Wand2 size={16} /></Button></div>
      </Field>
      <div className="flex items-end"><Button type="submit" disabled={busy} className="w-full sm:w-auto"><Plus /> {busy ? "Criando..." : "Cadastrar"}</Button></div>
      <ErrorNote text={error} />
      {created && <p className="rounded-lg bg-success-soft p-3 text-xs font-bold text-success sm:col-span-full">Conta criada. Envie ao novo membro: e-mail <span className="font-mono">{created.email}</span> Â· senha <span className="font-mono">{created.password}</span>. Recomende trocar a senha no primeiro acesso.</p>}
    </form>}
    <ul className="divide-y divide-border">
      {isLoading && <li className="p-6"><div className="h-10 animate-pulse rounded-lg bg-muted" /></li>}
      {members.map((m) => <li key={m.user_id} className="p-4 sm:px-5">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
          <span className="grid size-10 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full bg-navy-soft text-primary">{m.is_owner ? <Crown size={18} className="text-gold" /> : <UsersRound size={18} />}</span>
          <div className="min-w-0 flex-1"><p className="truncate font-bold">{m.email}</p><p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{m.is_owner ? "ResponsÃ¡vel pela plataforma" : `Desde ${formatDateTime(m.since)}`}</p></div>
          {isOwner && !m.is_owner && <div className="flex gap-2">
            <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={() => setResetting(resetting === m.user_id ? null : m.user_id)}><KeyRound size={15} /> Nova senha</Button>
            <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" className="text-destructive" onClick={() => remove(m.user_id, m.email)}><Trash2 size={15} /> Remover</Button>
          </div>}
        </div>
        {resetting === m.user_id && <ResetPassword userId={m.user_id} email={m.email} onDone={() => setResetting(null)} />}
      </li>)}
    </ul>
  </Panel>;
}

function ResetPassword({ userId, email, onDone }: { userId: string; email: string; onDone: () => void }) {
  const [password, setPassword] = useState(generatePassword);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState<{ ok: boolean; text: string } | null>(null);
  const save = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault(); setBusy(true); setMsg(null);
    const failure = await callAdminUsers({ action: "set_password", user_id: userId, password });
    setBusy(false);
    setMsg(failure.error ? { ok: false, text: failure.error } : { ok: true, text: `Senha de ${email} alterada para ${password}.` });
  };
  return <form onSubmit={save} className="mt-3 grid gap-2 rounded-lg border border-border bg-muted/50 p-3 sm:grid-cols-[1fr_auto_auto]">
    <input required minLength={8} value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} className={`${inputClass} font-mono`} aria-label="Nova senha" />
    <Button type="submit" disabled={busy}>{busy ? "Salvando..." : "Salvar senha"}</Button>
    <Button type="button" variant="ghost" onClick={onDone}>Fechar</Button>
    {msg && <p className={`rounded-lg p-2 text-xs font-bold sm:col-span-full ${msg.ok ? "bg-success-soft text-success" : "bg-destructive/10 text-destructive"}`}>{msg.text}</p>}
  </form>;
}


==================== src\components\admin\certificate-model.tsx ====================
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { ArrowLeft, Award, Download } from "lucide-react";
import { useState } from "react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
import { blankCertificate, downloadCertificate, templateSigners, type CertificateTemplateFields } from "@/lib/certificate";
import { ErrorNote, Field, ImageInput, Panel, inputClass } from "./form";

export function useCertificateTemplate() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ["admin", "certificate-template"],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.from("certificate_template").select("id, da_name, da_signature, coordinator_name, coordinator_signature, athletics_name, athletics_signature, updated_at").limit(1).maybeSingle();
      if (error) throw error;
      if (!data) throw new Error("missing template");
      return data;
    },
  });
}

const slots = [
  { role: "Presidente do DA", name: "da_name", signature: "da_signature" },
  { role: "Coordenador", name: "coordinator_name", signature: "coordinator_signature" },
  { role: "Presidente da AtlÃ©tica", name: "athletics_name", signature: "athletics_signature" },
] as const;

type SlotName = (typeof slots)[number]["name"];
type SlotSignature = (typeof slots)[number]["signature"];

export function CertificateModel({ onBack }: { onBack: () => void }) {
  const { data, isLoading, isError } = useCertificateTemplate();
  return <Panel title="Modelo do certificado" subtitle="Certificado em branco. As trÃªs assinaturas entram antes de liberar o evento para os alunos." action={<Button variant="outline" onClick={onBack}><ArrowLeft /> Voltar</Button>}>
    {isLoading && <div className="p-5"><div className="h-64 animate-pulse rounded-lg bg-muted" /></div>}
    {isError && <p className="p-5 text-sm text-destructive">NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel abrir o modelo.</p>}
    {data && <CertificateForm key={data.updated_at} template={data} />}
  </Panel>;
}

function CertificateForm({ template }: { template: CertificateTemplateFields & { id: string } }) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const [v, setV] = useState({
    da_name: template.da_name ?? "",
    da_signature: template.da_signature,
    coordinator_name: template.coordinator_name ?? "",
    coordinator_signature: template.coordinator_signature,
    athletics_name: template.athletics_name ?? "",
    athletics_signature: template.athletics_signature,
  });
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [previewing, setPreviewing] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false);
  const signers = templateSigners(v);
  const ready = signers.every((signer) => signer.name.length > 0 && !!signer.signature_url);
  const setName = (key: SlotName, value: string) => { setSaved(false); setV((cur) => ({ ...cur, [key]: value })); };
  const setSignature = (key: SlotSignature, value: string | null) => { setSaved(false); setV((cur) => ({ ...cur, [key]: value })); };

  const save = async () => {
    setBusy(true); setError(""); setSaved(false);
    const payload = {
      da_name: v.da_name.trim() || null,
      da_signature: v.da_signature,
      coordinator_name: v.coordinator_name.trim() || null,
      coordinator_signature: v.coordinator_signature,
      athletics_name: v.athletics_name.trim() || null,
      athletics_signature: v.athletics_signature,
      updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    };
    const { error: saveError } = await supabase.from("certificate_template").update(payload).eq("id", template.id);
    setBusy(false);
    if (saveError) return setError(saveError.code === "23514" ? "Cada nome pode ter no mÃ¡ximo 80 caracteres." : "NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel salvar o modelo.");
    setSaved(true);
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin", "certificate-template"] });
  };

  const preview = async () => {
    setPreviewing(true); setError("");
    try { await downloadCertificate(blankCertificate(signers)); }
    catch { setError("NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel gerar a prÃ©via. Tente novamente."); }
    setPreviewing(false);
  };

  return <>
    <div className="grid gap-4 border-b border-border p-5 lg:grid-cols-3">
      {slots.map((slot) => <div key={slot.role} className="grid content-start gap-3 rounded-lg border border-border bg-muted/40 p-4">
        <Field label={slot.role} hint="Nome como deve sair no certificado">
          <input maxLength={80} value={v[slot.name]} onChange={(e) => setName(slot.name, e.target.value)} placeholder="Nome completo" className={inputClass} />
        </Field>
        <ImageInput label="Assinatura" fit="contain" folder="signatures" value={v[slot.signature]} onChange={(url) => setSignature(slot.signature, url)} />
        <p className="-mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">PNG ou JPG, de preferÃªncia em fundo branco.</p>
      </div>)}
    </div>
    <div className="grid gap-4 p-5">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2"><Award size={16} className="text-gold" /><h3 className="font-bold">PrÃ©via do modelo</h3></div>
      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Os dados abaixo sÃ£o de exemplo. No certificado do aluno entram o nome, o RA, o evento e as horas escolhidas.</p>
      <CertificatePreview signers={signers} />
      <p className={`rounded-lg p-3 text-xs font-bold ${ready ? "bg-success-soft text-success" : "bg-muted text-muted-foreground"}`}>
        {ready ? "As trÃªs assinaturas estÃ£o no modelo. Salve e, no evento, marque Emitir certificado para presentes." : "Falta nome ou assinatura. Sem as trÃªs assinaturas o certificado nÃ£o vale e o aluno nÃ£o consegue baixar."}
      </p>
      {saved && <p className="rounded-lg bg-success-soft p-3 text-xs font-bold text-success">Modelo salvo.</p>}
      <ErrorNote text={error} />
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        <Button type="button" onClick={save} disabled={busy}>{busy ? "Salvando..." : "Salvar modelo"}</Button>
        <Button type="button" variant="outline" onClick={preview} disabled={previewing}><Download /> {previewing ? "Gerando..." : "Baixar prÃ©via"}</Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </>;
}

function CertificatePreview({ signers }: { signers: ReturnType<typeof templateSigners> }) {
  return <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-xl bg-neutral-900 p-3 sm:p-4">
    <div className="bg-white px-4 py-5 text-center text-neutral-900 ring-1 ring-gold sm:px-8">
      <p className="text-[10px] font-bold tracking-[0.22em] text-gold">DIRETÃ“RIO ACADÃŠMICO DE DIREITO</p>
      <h3 className="mt-2 font-serif text-2xl font-bold tracking-[0.18em] sm:text-3xl">CERTIFICADO</h3>
      <div className="mx-auto mt-2 h-0.5 w-20 bg-gold" />
      <p className="mt-4 text-xs text-neutral-500">Certificamos que</p>
      <p className="mt-1 font-serif text-xl font-bold sm:text-2xl">Nome do aluno</p>
      <p className="mx-auto mt-3 max-w-xl text-xs leading-relaxed">participou do evento â€œNome do eventoâ€, realizado em 3â€“6 de novembro de 2026, em Local do evento, com carga horÃ¡ria de 10 horas complementares.</p>
      <div className="mt-6 grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 sm:gap-4">
        {signers.map((signer) => <div key={signer.role} className="grid justify-items-center">
          <span className="grid h-12 w-full place-items-center">{signer.signature_url ? <img src={signer.signature_url} alt="" className="max-h-12 max-w-full object-contain" /> : <span className="text-[10px] text-neutral-300">Assinatura</span>}</span>
          <span className="mt-1 h-px w-full max-w-28 bg-neutral-800" />
          <span className="mt-1 max-w-full truncate text-[11px] font-bold">{signer.name || "Nome"}</span>
          <span className="text-[10px] text-neutral-500">{signer.role}</span>
        </div>)}
      </div>
      <p className="mt-4 text-[10px] text-neutral-400">RA 0000000</p>
    </div>
  </div>;
}


==================== src\components\admin\event-photos.tsx ====================
import { useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { ArrowLeft, ImagePlus, Loader2, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import { useState } from "react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
import { type EventRow, uploadMedia, useEventPhotos } from "@/lib/data";
import { ErrorNote, Panel } from "./form";

const accepted = ["image/jpeg", "image/png", "image/webp"];

async function preparePhoto(file: File) {
  if (!accepted.includes(file.type)) throw new Error("type");
  const bitmap = await createImageBitmap(file);
  const scale = Math.min(1, 1600 / Math.max(bitmap.width, bitmap.height));
  const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
  canvas.width = Math.max(1, Math.round(bitmap.width * scale));
  canvas.height = Math.max(1, Math.round(bitmap.height * scale));
  const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
  if (!ctx) throw new Error("size");
  ctx.drawImage(bitmap, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
  bitmap.close();
  let blob: Blob | null = null;
  for (let quality = 0.82; quality >= 0.5; quality -= 0.1) {
    blob = await new Promise((resolve) => canvas.toBlob(resolve, "image/webp", quality));
    if (blob && blob.size <= 1.8 * 1024 * 1024) break;
  }
  if (!blob || blob.size > 2 * 1024 * 1024) throw new Error("size");
  return new File([blob], file.name.replace(/\.\w+$/, "") + ".webp", { type: "image/webp" });
}

export function EventPhotos({ event, onBack }: { event: EventRow; onBack: () => void }) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const { data: photos = [], isLoading } = useEventPhotos(event.id);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [progress, setProgress] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const refresh = () => {
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["events", "photos", event.id] });
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin", "event-photos"] });
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin", "activity"] });
  };

  const add = async (list: FileList | null) => {
    const files = [...(list ?? [])];
    if (!files.length) return;
    setBusy(true); setError("");
    let failed = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < files.length; i++) {
      const file = files[i];
      if (!file) continue;
      setProgress(`Enviando ${i + 1} de ${files.length}...`);
      try {
        const image = await preparePhoto(file);
        const image_url = await uploadMedia(image, "gallery");
        const { error: insertError } = await supabase.from("event_photos").insert({ event_id: event.id, image_url });
        if (insertError) throw insertError;
      } catch { failed += 1; }
    }
    setBusy(false); setProgress("");
    if (failed) setError(failed === files.length ? "NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel enviar as fotos. Use JPG, PNG ou WEBP." : `${failed} foto${failed === 1 ? "" : "s"} nÃ£o ${failed === 1 ? "foi enviada" : "foram enviadas"}. As outras jÃ¡ estÃ£o na galeria.`);
    refresh();
  };

  const remove = async (id: string, imageUrl: string) => {
    if (!window.confirm("Remover esta foto da galeria do evento?")) return;
    const { error: deleteError } = await supabase.from("event_photos").delete().eq("id", id);
    if (deleteError) return setError("NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel remover a foto.");
    const path = decodeURIComponent(imageUrl.split("/object/public/media/")[1] ?? "");
    if (path) await supabase.storage.from("media").remove([path]);
    refresh();
  };

  return <Panel title={`Fotos Â· ${event.title}`} subtitle={event.published ? "Os alunos veem e baixam estas fotos na pÃ¡gina do evento." : "Este evento ainda Ã© rascunho. As fotos sÃ³ aparecem para os alunos depois que ele for publicado."} action={<Button variant="outline" onClick={onBack}><ArrowLeft /> Voltar</Button>}>
    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 border-b border-border p-4 sm:px-5">
      <label className={`inline-flex h-10 cursor-pointer items-center gap-2 rounded-lg bg-primary px-3 text-sm font-bold text-primary-foreground ${busy ? "pointer-events-none opacity-60" : ""}`}>
        {busy ? <Loader2 size={16} className="animate-spin" /> : <ImagePlus size={16} />}{progress || "Enviar fotos"}
        <input type="file" accept="image/jpeg,image/png,image/webp" multiple className="sr-only" disabled={busy} onChange={(e) => { add(e.target.files); e.target.value = ""; }} />
      </label>
      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">JPG, PNG ou WEBP. VÃ¡rias de uma vez. Fotos grandes sÃ£o reduzidas automaticamente.</p>
    </div>
    <div className="p-4 sm:p-5">
      <ErrorNote text={error} />
      {isLoading && <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-4"><div className="aspect-square animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-muted" /></div>}
      {!isLoading && photos.length === 0 && <p className="rounded-lg bg-muted p-6 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">Nenhuma foto neste evento.</p>}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-4">
        {photos.map((photo, index) => <div key={photo.id} className="relative overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-border">
          <img src={photo.image_url} alt="" loading="lazy" className="aspect-square w-full object-cover" />
          <button type="button" className="absolute right-2 top-2 grid size-8 place-items-center rounded-lg bg-card text-destructive" onClick={() => remove(photo.id, photo.image_url)} aria-label={`Remover foto ${index + 1}`}><Trash2 size={15} /></button>
        </div>)}
      </div>
    </div>
  </Panel>;
}


==================== src\components\admin\events-admin.tsx ====================
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { format } from "date-fns";
import { ArrowLeft, Award, CalendarDays, Download, Eye, EyeOff, Images, Pencil, Plus, Search, Star, Trash2, UsersRound } from "lucide-react";
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
import { templateReady } from "@/lib/certificate";
import { type EventCategory, type EventRow, eventCategories, formatDateTime, formatEventDays, slugify } from "@/lib/data";
import { CertificateModel, useCertificateTemplate } from "./certificate-model";
import { EventPhotos } from "./event-photos";
import { ErrorNote, Field, ImageInput, Panel, Toggle, inputClass } from "./form";

type AdminEvent = EventRow & { registrations: { count: number }[] };

function useAdminEvents() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ["admin", "events"],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.from("events").select("*, registrations(count)").order("starts_at", { ascending: false });
      if (error) throw error;
      return data as AdminEvent[];
    },
  });
}

export function EventsAdmin() {
  const { data: events = [], isLoading } = useAdminEvents();
  const [form, setForm] = useState<{ mode: "new" } | { mode: "edit"; event: EventRow } | null>(null);
  const [attendees, setAttendees] = useState<EventRow | null>(null);
  const [gallery, setGallery] = useState<EventRow | null>(null);
  const [model, setModel] = useState(false);
  const { data: photoRows = [] } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["admin", "event-photos"],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.from("event_photos").select("event_id");
      if (error) throw error;
      return data;
    },
  });
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();

  if (attendees) return <Attendees event={attendees} onBack={() => setAttendees(null)} />;
  if (gallery) return <EventPhotos event={gallery} onBack={() => setGallery(null)} />;
  if (model) return <CertificateModel onBack={() => setModel(false)} />;

  const remove = async (e: EventRow) => {
    if (!window.confirm(`Excluir "${e.title}"? As inscriÃ§Ãµes e as fotos deste evento tambÃ©m serÃ£o apagadas.`)) return;
    await supabase.from("events").delete().eq("id", e.id);
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin"] });
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["events"] });
  };

  return <Panel title="Eventos" subtitle="Divulgue eventos e acompanhe os inscritos." action={<div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">{!form && <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => setModel(true)}><Award /> Modelo do certificado</Button>}<Button onClick={() => setForm({ mode: "new" })}><Plus /> Novo evento</Button></div>}>
    {form && <EventForm key={form.mode === "edit" ? form.event.id : "new"} initial={form.mode === "edit" ? form.event : null} onDone={() => setForm(null)} />}
    <ul className="divide-y divide-border">
      {isLoading && <li className="p-6"><div className="h-12 animate-pulse rounded-lg bg-muted" /></li>}
      {events.map((e) => {
        const count = e.registrations[0]?.count ?? 0;
        const photoCount = photoRows.filter((photo) => photo.event_id === e.id).length;
        return <li key={e.id} className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 p-4 sm:flex-nowrap sm:gap-4 sm:px-5">
          {e.image_url ? <img src={e.image_url} alt="" className="size-12 shrink-0 rounded-lg object-cover grayscale" /> : <span className="grid size-12 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-lg bg-muted text-muted-foreground"><CalendarDays size={20} /></span>}
          <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
            <p className="flex items-center gap-1.5 truncate font-bold">{e.featured && <Star size={14} className="shrink-0 fill-gold text-gold" />}{e.title}</p>
            <p className="truncate text-xs text-muted-foreground">{formatEventDays(e)} Â· {count}{e.capacity ? `/${e.capacity}` : ""} inscritos</p>
            <div className="mt-1 flex gap-1.5">
              <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase ${e.published ? "bg-success-soft text-success" : "bg-muted text-muted-foreground"}`}>{e.published ? <Eye size={11} /> : <EyeOff size={11} />}{e.published ? "Publicado" : "Rascunho"}</span>
              {!e.registration_open && <span className="rounded-full bg-muted px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase text-muted-foreground">InscriÃ§Ãµes fechadas</span>}
              {e.certificate_enabled && <span className="rounded-full bg-navy-soft px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase text-primary">Certificado Â· {formatHours(e.certificate_complementary_hours ?? e.certificate_hours)}h compl.{e.certificate_mandatory_hours != null ? ` Â· ${formatHours(e.certificate_mandatory_hours)}h obrig.` : ""}</span>}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="flex w-full gap-2 sm:w-auto">
            <Button variant="outline" size="sm" className="flex-1 sm:flex-none" onClick={() => setGallery(e)}><Images size={16} /> Fotos{photoCount ? ` Â· ${photoCount}` : ""}</Button>
            <Button variant="outline" size="sm" className="flex-1 sm:flex-none" onClick={() => setAttendees(e)}><UsersRound size={16} /> Inscritos</Button>
            <Button variant="outline" size="icon" onClick={() => setForm({ mode: "edit", event: e })} aria-label={`Editar ${e.title}`}><Pencil size={16} /></Button>
            <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" className="text-destructive" onClick={() => remove(e)} aria-label={`Excluir ${e.title}`}><Trash2 size={16} /></Button>
          </div>
        </li>;
      })}
      {!isLoading && events.length === 0 && <li className="p-6 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">Nenhum evento cadastrado.</li>}
    </ul>
  </Panel>;
}

const formatHours = (value: number | null) => (value == null ? "" : String(value).replace(".", ","));
const parseHours = (value: string) => {
  const trimmed = value.trim();
  if (!trimmed) return null;
  return Number.parseFloat(trimmed.replace(",", "."));
};
const toLocalInput = (iso: string | null) => (iso ? format(new Date(iso), "yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm") : "");
const fromLocalInput = (value: string) => (value ? new Date(value).toISOString() : null);

function EventForm({ initial, onDone }: { initial: EventRow | null; onDone: () => void }) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const { data: schedule } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["admin", "schedule", initial?.id],
    enabled: !!initial,
    queryFn: async () => { const { data } = await supabase.from("event_schedule").select("*").eq("event_id", initial!.id).order("sort_order"); return data ?? []; },
  });
  const [v, setV] = useState({
    title: initial?.title ?? "",
    slug: initial?.slug ?? "",
    summary: initial?.summary ?? "",
    description: initial?.description ?? "",
    location: initial?.location ?? "",
    starts_at: toLocalInput(initial?.starts_at ?? null),
    ends_at: toLocalInput(initial?.ends_at ?? null),
    capacity: initial?.capacity?.toString() ?? "",
    image_url: initial?.image_url ?? null,
    published: initial?.published ?? false,
    featured: initial?.featured ?? false,
    registration_open: initial?.registration_open ?? true,
    category: initial?.category ?? ("faculdade" as EventCategory),
    registration_closes_at: toLocalInput(initial?.registration_closes_at ?? null),
    certificate_enabled: initial?.certificate_enabled ?? false,
    certificate_complementary_hours: formatHours(initial?.certificate_complementary_hours ?? initial?.certificate_hours ?? null),
    certificate_mandatory_hours: formatHours(initial?.certificate_mandatory_hours ?? null),
  });
  const [scheduleText, setScheduleText] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const templateQuery = useCertificateTemplate();
  const scheduleValue = scheduleText ?? (schedule ?? []).map((s) => s.label).join("\n");
  const set = <K extends keyof typeof v>(key: K, value: (typeof v)[K]) => setV((cur) => ({ ...cur, [key]: value }));

  const save = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const complementary = parseHours(v.certificate_complementary_hours);
    const mandatory = parseHours(v.certificate_mandatory_hours);
    if ((complementary != null && (!Number.isFinite(complementary) || complementary <= 0)) || (mandatory != null && (!Number.isFinite(mandatory) || mandatory <= 0))) return setError("Informe as horas com um nÃºmero maior que zero, ex.: 3 ou 1,5.");
    if (v.certificate_enabled && (complementary == null || mandatory == null)) return setError("Informe as horas complementares e as obrigatÃ³rias. Os dois valores podem ser diferentes.");
    if (v.certificate_enabled && !initial?.certificate_enabled) {
      if (templateQuery.isLoading) return setError("Aguarde o modelo do certificado carregar.");
      if (!templateReady(templateQuery.data)) return setError("Salve o modelo do certificado com o nome e a assinatura do presidente do DA, do coordenador e do presidente da AtlÃ©tica.");
    }
    setBusy(true); setError("");
    const payload = {
      category: v.category,
      registration_closes_at: fromLocalInput(v.registration_closes_at),
      certificate_enabled: v.certificate_enabled,
      certificate_complementary_hours: complementary,
      certificate_mandatory_hours: mandatory,
      certificate_hours: complementary,
      title: v.title.trim(),
      slug: slugify(v.slug || v.title),
      summary: v.summary.trim() || null,
      description: v.description.trim() || null,
      location: v.location.trim() || null,
      starts_at: fromLocalInput(v.starts_at)!,
      ends_at: fromLocalInput(v.ends_at),
      capacity: v.capacity ? Number.parseInt(v.capacity, 10) : null,
      image_url: v.image_url,
      published: v.published,
      featured: v.featured,
      registration_open: v.registration_open,
    };
    const res = initial
      ? await supabase.from("events").update(payload).eq("id", initial.id).select("id").single()
      : await supabase.from("events").insert(payload).select("id").single();
    if (res.error) {
      setBusy(false);
      const constraint = res.error.code === "23514" ? res.error.message : "";
      return setError(constraint.includes("certificate_signers_missing")
        ? "Salve o modelo do certificado com as trÃªs assinaturas antes de liberar."
        : constraint.includes("certificate_not_ready")
        ? "Informe as horas e conclua as assinaturas antes de liberar o certificado."
        : res.error.code === "23505" ? "JÃ¡ existe um evento com esse endereÃ§o (slug)." : res.error.code === "23514" ? "Confira as datas: o tÃ©rmino deve ser depois do inÃ­cio." : "NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel salvar o evento.");
    }
    if (scheduleText !== null) {
      const eventId = res.data.id;
      await supabase.from("event_schedule").delete().eq("event_id", eventId);
      const rows = scheduleText.split("\n").map((l) => l.trim()).filter(Boolean).map((label, i) => ({ event_id: eventId, label, sort_order: i + 1 }));
      if (rows.length) await supabase.from("event_schedule").insert(rows);
    }
    setBusy(false);
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin"] });
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["events"] });
    onDone();
  };

  return <form onSubmit={save} className="grid gap-4 border-b border-border bg-muted/50 p-5 sm:grid-cols-2">
    <Field label="TÃ­tulo"><input required value={v.title} onChange={(e) => set("title", e.target.value)} onBlur={() => !v.slug && set("slug", slugify(v.title))} placeholder="Semana JurÃ­dica 2026" className={inputClass} /></Field>
    <Field label="EndereÃ§o da pÃ¡gina" hint={`/eventos/${slugify(v.slug || v.title) || "..."}`}><input value={v.slug} onChange={(e) => set("slug", e.target.value)} placeholder="semana-juridica-2026" className={inputClass} /></Field>
    <Field label="InÃ­cio"><input required type="datetime-local" value={v.starts_at} onChange={(e) => set("starts_at", e.target.value)} className={inputClass} /></Field>
    <Field label="TÃ©rmino" hint="opcional"><input type="datetime-local" value={v.ends_at} onChange={(e) => set("ends_at", e.target.value)} className={inputClass} /></Field>
    <Field label="Categoria"><select value={v.category} onChange={(e) => set("category", e.target.value as EventCategory)} className={inputClass}>{eventCategories.map((c) => <option key={c.id} value={c.id}>{c.label}</option>)}</select></Field>
    <Field label="InscriÃ§Ãµes atÃ©" hint="opcional; depois disso o formulÃ¡rio fecha sozinho"><input type="datetime-local" value={v.registration_closes_at} onChange={(e) => set("registration_closes_at", e.target.value)} className={inputClass} /></Field>
    <Field label="Local"><input value={v.location} onChange={(e) => set("location", e.target.value)} placeholder="AuditÃ³rio Central" className={inputClass} /></Field>
    <Field label="Vagas" hint="deixe vazio para ilimitado"><input type="number" min={1} value={v.capacity} onChange={(e) => set("capacity", e.target.value)} className={inputClass} /></Field>
    <Field label="Resumo" hint="aparece no destaque da Home" className="sm:col-span-2"><input maxLength={200} value={v.summary} onChange={(e) => set("summary", e.target.value)} className={inputClass} /></Field>
    <Field label="DescriÃ§Ã£o" className="sm:col-span-2"><textarea rows={4} value={v.description} onChange={(e) => set("description", e.target.value)} className={`${inputClass} h-auto py-2`} /></Field>
    <Field label="ProgramaÃ§Ã£o" hint="um item por linha, ex.: 23 nov Â· Abertura" className="sm:col-span-2"><textarea rows={3} value={scheduleValue} onChange={(e) => setScheduleText(e.target.value)} className={`${inputClass} h-auto py-2`} /></Field>
    <ImageInput value={v.image_url} onChange={(url) => set("image_url", url)} folder="events" />
    <div className="grid content-start gap-2.5 pt-1">
      <Toggle label="Publicado no site" checked={v.published} onChange={(x) => set("published", x)} />
      <Toggle label="InscriÃ§Ãµes abertas" checked={v.registration_open} onChange={(x) => set("registration_open", x)} />
      <Toggle label="Destaque na Home" checked={v.featured} onChange={(x) => set("featured", x)} />
      <Toggle label="Emitir certificado para presentes" checked={v.certificate_enabled} onChange={(x) => { if (x && !templateReady(templateQuery.data)) { setError("Salve o modelo do certificado com as trÃªs assinaturas antes de liberar."); return; } setError(""); set("certificate_enabled", x); }} />
      <p className="text-xs font-normal text-muted-foreground">A liberaÃ§Ã£o copia o modelo assinado pelo presidente do DA, pelo coordenador e pelo presidente da AtlÃ©tica. Sem essas assinaturas o certificado nÃ£o vale.</p>
    </div>
    <Field label="Horas complementares" hint="Do 1Âº ao 3Âº ano o certificado usa sÃ³ este valor. A liberaÃ§Ã£o vale depois do tÃ©rmino do evento."><input inputMode="decimal" value={v.certificate_complementary_hours} onChange={(e) => set("certificate_complementary_hours", e.target.value)} placeholder="10" className={inputClass} /></Field>
    <Field label="Horas obrigatÃ³rias" hint="OpÃ§Ã£o do 4Âº e do 5Âº ano. Pode ser diferente das complementares."><input inputMode="decimal" value={v.certificate_mandatory_hours} onChange={(e) => set("certificate_mandatory_hours", e.target.value)} placeholder="8" className={inputClass} /></Field>
    <ErrorNote text={error} />
    <div className="flex gap-2 sm:col-span-2"><Button type="submit" disabled={busy}>{busy ? "Salvando..." : "Salvar evento"}</Button><Button type="button" variant="ghost" onClick={onDone}>Cancelar</Button></div>
  </form>;
}

function Attendees({ event, onBack }: { event: EventRow; onBack: () => void }) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const [q, setQ] = useState("");
  const { data: rows = [], isLoading } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["admin", "registrations", event.id],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.from("registrations").select("*").eq("event_id", event.id).order("created_at");
      if (error) throw error;
      return data;
    },
  });
  const visible = useMemo(() => {
    const term = q.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!term) return rows;
    return rows.filter((r) => r.full_name.toLowerCase().includes(term) || r.email.includes(term) || r.ra.toLowerCase().includes(term));
  }, [rows, q]);
  const checkedIn = rows.filter((r) => r.checked_in_at).length;
  const refresh = () => { queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin"] }); };

  const toggleCheckIn = async (id: string, value: boolean) => {
    await supabase.from("registrations").update({ checked_in_at: value ? new Date().toISOString() : null }).eq("id", id);
    refresh();
  };
  const remove = async (id: string, name: string) => {
    if (!window.confirm(`Remover a inscriÃ§Ã£o de ${name}?`)) return;
    await supabase.from("registrations").delete().eq("id", id);
    refresh();
  };
  const exportCsv = () => {
    const header = ["Nome", "E-mail", "RA", "Inscrito em", "PresenÃ§a"];
    const lines = rows.map((r) => [r.full_name, r.email, r.ra, formatDateTime(r.created_at), r.checked_in_at ? "Presente" : "Ausente"]);
    const csv = [header, ...lines].map((cols) => cols.map((c) => `"${String(c).replace(/"/g, '""')}"`).join(";")).join("\r\n");
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob(["\uFEFF" + csv], { type: "text/csv;charset=utf-8" }));
    const a = Object.assign(document.createElement("a"), { href: url, download: `inscritos-${event.slug}.csv` });
    a.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  };

  return <Panel title={`Inscritos Â· ${event.title}`} subtitle={`${rows.length}${event.capacity ? ` de ${event.capacity} vagas` : " inscritos"} Â· ${checkedIn} presenÃ§a${checkedIn === 1 ? "" : "s"} confirmada${checkedIn === 1 ? "" : "s"}`}
    action={<div className="flex gap-2"><Button variant="outline" onClick={onBack}><ArrowLeft /> Voltar</Button><Button onClick={exportCsv} disabled={!rows.length}><Download /> Exportar CSV</Button></div>}>
    <div className="border-b border-border p-4 sm:px-5"><div className="relative"><Search size={16} className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted-foreground" /><input value={q} onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)} placeholder="Buscar por nome, e-mail ou RA" className={`${inputClass} pl-9`} /></div></div>
    <div className="overflow-x-auto">
      <table className="w-full min-w-[640px] text-left text-sm">
        <thead className="bg-muted text-xs uppercase text-muted-foreground"><tr><th className="px-5 py-3">Nome</th><th className="px-5 py-3">E-mail</th><th className="px-5 py-3">RA</th><th className="px-5 py-3">PresenÃ§a</th><th className="px-5 py-3" /></tr></thead>
        <tbody>
          {isLoading && <tr><td colSpan={5} className="px-5 py-8"><div className="h-8 animate-pulse rounded bg-muted" /></td></tr>}
          {visible.map((r) => <tr key={r.id} className="border-t border-border">
            <td className="px-5 py-4 font-bold">{r.full_name}<p className="text-xs font-normal text-muted-foreground">{formatDateTime(r.created_at)}</p></td>
            <td className="px-5 py-4 text-muted-foreground">{r.email}</td>
            <td className="px-5 py-4 font-mono text-xs font-bold">{r.ra}</td>
            <td className="px-5 py-4"><Toggle label={r.checked_in_at ? "Presente" : "Ausente"} checked={!!r.checked_in_at} onChange={(x) => toggleCheckIn(r.id, x)} /></td>
            <td className="px-5 py-4 text-right"><Button variant="ghost" size="icon" className="text-destructive" onClick={() => remove(r.id, r.full_name)} aria-label={`Remover ${r.full_name}`}><Trash2 size={16} /></Button></td>
          </tr>)}
          {!isLoading && visible.length === 0 && <tr><td colSpan={5} className="px-5 py-8 text-center text-muted-foreground">{rows.length ? "Nenhum inscrito encontrado." : "Ainda nÃ£o hÃ¡ inscritos neste evento."}</td></tr>}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  </Panel>;
}


==================== src\components\admin\form.tsx ====================
import { ImagePlus, Loader2 } from "lucide-react";
import { useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { type MediaFolder, uploadMedia } from "@/lib/data";

export const inputClass = "h-11 w-full rounded-lg border border-input bg-background px-3 font-normal outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-ring";

async function compressToWebP(file: File, maxDimension = 1400): Promise<File> {
  if (!["image/jpeg", "image/png", "image/webp"].includes(file.type)) {
    throw new Error("Formato invÃ¡lido");
  }
  const bitmap = await createImageBitmap(file);
  const scale = Math.min(1, maxDimension / Math.max(bitmap.width, bitmap.height));
  const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
  canvas.width = Math.max(1, Math.round(bitmap.width * scale));
  canvas.height = Math.max(1, Math.round(bitmap.height * scale));
  const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
  if (!ctx) throw new Error("Canvas indisponÃ­vel");
  ctx.drawImage(bitmap, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
  bitmap.close();

  const blob = await new Promise<Blob | null>((resolve) =>
    canvas.toBlob(resolve, "image/webp", 0.82)
  );
  if (!blob || blob.size > 2 * 1024 * 1024) {
    throw new Error("Imagem muito grande mesmo apÃ³s compressÃ£o");
  }
  return new File([blob], file.name.replace(/\.\w+$/, "") + ".webp", { type: "image/webp" });
}

export function Field({ label, hint, className = "", children }: { label: string; hint?: string; className?: string; children: ReactNode }) {
  return <label className={`grid gap-1.5 text-sm font-bold ${className}`}>{label}{hint && <span className="-mt-1 text-xs font-normal text-muted-foreground">{hint}</span>}{children}</label>;
}

export function Toggle({ label, checked, onChange }: { label: string; checked: boolean; onChange: (v: boolean) => void }) {
  return <label className="flex items-center gap-2.5 text-sm font-bold"><input type="checkbox" checked={checked} onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.checked)} className="size-4 accent-gold" />{label}</label>;
}

export function ImageInput({ value, onChange, folder, label = "Imagem", fit = "cover" }: { value: string | null; onChange: (url: string | null) => void; folder: MediaFolder; label?: string; fit?: "cover" | "contain" }) {
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const pick = async (file: File | undefined) => {
    if (!file) return;
    if (file.size > 15 * 1024 * 1024) return setError("A imagem original deve ter no mÃ¡ximo 15 MB.");
    setBusy(true); setError("");
    try {
      // Assinaturas precisam manter transparÃªncia PNG se enviada; demais imagens viram WebP leve
      const processed = folder === "signatures" && file.type === "image/png" && file.size <= 1024 * 1024
        ? file
        : await compressToWebP(file, folder === "signatures" ? 800 : 1400);
      onChange(await uploadMedia(processed, folder));
    } catch {
      setError("NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel enviar a imagem. Use JPG, PNG ou WEBP.");
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };
  return <div className="grid gap-1.5 text-sm font-bold">{label}
    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
      {value ? <img src={value} alt="" className={fit === "contain" ? "h-16 w-28 shrink-0 rounded-lg border border-border bg-white object-contain" : "size-16 shrink-0 rounded-lg object-cover"} /> : <span className={`grid shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-lg bg-muted text-muted-foreground ${fit === "contain" ? "h-16 w-28" : "size-16"}`}><ImagePlus size={20} /></span>}
      <label className="inline-flex h-10 cursor-pointer items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-border bg-background px-3 text-sm font-bold hover:bg-muted">
        {busy ? <Loader2 size={16} className="animate-spin" /> : <ImagePlus size={16} />}{value ? "Trocar" : "Enviar"}
        <input type="file" accept="image/jpeg,image/png,image/webp" className="sr-only" disabled={busy} onChange={(e) => { pick(e.target.files?.[0]); e.target.value = ""; }} />
      </label>
      {value && <button type="button" className="text-xs font-bold text-destructive" onClick={() => onChange(null)}>Remover</button>}
    </div>
    {error && <span className="text-xs text-destructive">{error}</span>}
  </div>;
}

export function Panel({ title, subtitle, action, children }: { title: string; subtitle?: string; action?: ReactNode; children: ReactNode }) {
  return <section className="mt-6 rounded-xl border border-border bg-card">
    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-border p-5"><div><h2 className="text-lg font-bold">{title}</h2>{subtitle && <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{subtitle}</p>}</div>{action}</div>
    {children}
  </section>;
}

export function ErrorNote({ text }: { text: string }) {
  return text ? <p className="rounded-lg bg-destructive/10 p-3 text-xs font-bold text-destructive sm:col-span-full">{text}</p> : null;
}

==================== src\components\admin\members-admin.tsx ====================
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { ArrowDown, ArrowUp, Pencil, Plus, Trash2, UserRound, Wand2 } from "lucide-react";
import { useState } from "react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
import type { BoardMemberRow } from "@/lib/data";
import { callAdminUsers, generatePassword } from "./admin-users";
import { ErrorNote, Field, ImageInput, Panel, Toggle, inputClass } from "./form";

type Account = { user_id: string; email: string; is_owner: boolean };

export function MembersAdmin({ isOwner }: { isOwner: boolean }) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const { data: members = [], isLoading } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["admin", "board-members"],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.from("board_members").select("*").order("sort_order").order("created_at");
      if (error) throw error;
      return data;
    },
  });
  const { data: accounts = [] } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["board"],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("list_board");
      if (error) throw error;
      return data;
    },
  });
  const [form, setForm] = useState<{ mode: "new" } | { mode: "edit"; member: BoardMemberRow } | null>(null);
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState("");
  const refresh = () => {
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin"] });
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["board"] });
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["board-members"] });
  };
  const emailOf = (userId: string | null) => accounts.find((account) => account.user_id === userId)?.email ?? null;

  const remove = async (m: BoardMemberRow) => {
    const email = m.user_id ? emailOf(m.user_id) : null;
    if (m.user_id && !isOwner) return window.alert("SÃ³ o responsÃ¡vel pode excluir um membro que tem acesso ao painel.");
    const message = email ? `Excluir ${m.name}? O acesso ${email} tambÃ©m serÃ¡ apagado.` : `Excluir ${m.name} da diretoria?`;
    if (!window.confirm(message)) return;
    if (m.user_id) {
      const failure = await callAdminUsers({ action: "delete", user_id: m.user_id });
      if (failure.error) return window.alert(failure.error);
    }
    await supabase.from("board_members").delete().eq("id", m.id);
    refresh();
  };

  const chapa = members.filter((m) => m.in_chapa);
  const others = members.filter((m) => !m.in_chapa);
  const moveWithin = async (inChapa: boolean, index: number, delta: -1 | 1) => {
    const group = inChapa ? chapa : others;
    const target = index + delta;
    if (target < 0 || target >= group.length) return;
    const next = [...group];
    const [item] = next.splice(index, 1);
    next.splice(target, 0, item!);
    const ordered = inChapa ? [...next, ...others] : [...chapa, ...next];
    await Promise.all(ordered.map((m, i) => (m.sort_order === i + 1 ? null : supabase.from("board_members").update({ sort_order: i + 1 }).eq("id", m.id))));
    refresh();
  };

  const nextOrder = members.reduce((max, m) => Math.max(max, m.sort_order), 0) + 1;
  const sectors = [...new Set(members.map((m) => m.sector))];

  return <Panel title="Membros do DA" subtitle="A chapa fica separada dos demais integrantes. Quem nÃ£o faz parte da chapa pode ser ocultado ou removido sem alterar a chapa." action={<Button onClick={() => { setNotice(""); setForm({ mode: "new" }); }}><Plus /> Novo membro</Button>}>
    {notice && <p className="border-b border-border bg-success-soft p-4 text-xs font-bold text-success">{notice}</p>}
    {form && <MemberForm key={form.mode === "edit" ? form.member.id : "new"} initial={form.mode === "edit" ? form.member : null} nextOrder={nextOrder} sectors={sectors} isOwner={isOwner} linkedEmail={form.mode === "edit" ? emailOf(form.member.user_id) : null} accounts={accounts} members={members} onDone={(message) => { setForm(null); setNotice(message ?? ""); refresh(); }} onCancel={() => setForm(null)} />}
    {isLoading && <div className="p-6"><div className="h-12 animate-pulse rounded-lg bg-muted" /></div>}
    {!isLoading && members.length === 0 && <p className="p-6 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">Nenhum membro cadastrado.</p>}
    {!isLoading && members.length > 0 && <>
      <MemberList title="Chapa" members={chapa} empty="Nenhum membro na chapa." onMove={(index, delta) => moveWithin(true, index, delta)} onEdit={(m) => { setNotice(""); setForm({ mode: "edit", member: m }); }} onRemove={remove} emailOf={emailOf} />
      <MemberList title="Demais integrantes" members={others} empty="Nenhum integrante fora da chapa." onMove={(index, delta) => moveWithin(false, index, delta)} onEdit={(m) => { setNotice(""); setForm({ mode: "edit", member: m }); }} onRemove={remove} emailOf={emailOf} />
    </>}
  </Panel>;
}

function MemberList({ title, members, empty, onMove, onEdit, onRemove, emailOf }: { title: string; members: BoardMemberRow[]; empty: string; onMove: (index: number, delta: -1 | 1) => void; onEdit: (member: BoardMemberRow) => void; onRemove: (member: BoardMemberRow) => void; emailOf: (userId: string | null) => string | null }) {
  return <section className="border-t border-border">
    <h3 className="px-5 pt-4 text-sm font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-gold">{title}</h3>
    {members.length === 0 ? <p className="px-5 py-4 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{empty}</p> : <ul className="divide-y divide-border">
      {members.map((m, i) => <li key={m.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 p-4 sm:gap-4 sm:px-5">
        {m.photo_url ? <img src={m.photo_url} alt="" className="size-12 shrink-0 rounded-lg object-cover" /> : <span className="grid size-12 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-lg bg-muted text-muted-foreground"><UserRound size={20} /></span>}
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <p className="truncate font-bold">{m.name}</p>
          <p className="truncate text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-gold">{m.role}</p>
          <p className="truncate text-xs text-muted-foreground">{m.sector}{m.instagram && ` Â· @${m.instagram}`} Â· {emailOf(m.user_id) ?? "Sem acesso ao painel"}</p>
          {!m.active && <span className="mt-1 inline-flex rounded-full bg-muted px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase text-muted-foreground">Oculto</span>}
        </div>
        <div className="flex shrink-0 gap-1">
          <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" disabled={i === 0} onClick={() => onMove(i, -1)} aria-label={`Subir ${m.name}`}><ArrowUp size={16} /></Button>
          <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" disabled={i === members.length - 1} onClick={() => onMove(i, 1)} aria-label={`Descer ${m.name}`}><ArrowDown size={16} /></Button>
          <Button variant="outline" size="icon" onClick={() => onEdit(m)} aria-label={`Editar ${m.name}`}><Pencil size={16} /></Button>
          <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" className="text-destructive" onClick={() => onRemove(m)} aria-label={`Excluir ${m.name}`}><Trash2 size={16} /></Button>
        </div>
      </li>)}
    </ul>}
  </section>;
}

const cleanInstagram = (value: string) => value.trim().replace(/^https?:\/\/(www\.)?instagram\.com\//i, "").replace(/^@/, "").replace(/\/.*$/, "");

function MemberForm({ initial, nextOrder, sectors, isOwner, linkedEmail, accounts, members, onDone, onCancel }: { initial: BoardMemberRow | null; nextOrder: number; sectors: string[]; isOwner: boolean; linkedEmail: string | null; accounts: Account[]; members: BoardMemberRow[]; onDone: (notice?: string) => void; onCancel: () => void }) {
  const [name, setName] = useState(initial?.name ?? "");
  const [role, setRole] = useState(initial?.role ?? "");
  const [sector, setSector] = useState(initial?.sector ?? sectors[0] ?? "Diretoria");
  const [instagram, setInstagram] = useState(initial?.instagram ?? "");
  const [photo, setPhoto] = useState<string | null>(initial?.photo_url ?? null);
  const [active, setActive] = useState(initial?.active ?? true);
  const [inChapa, setInChapa] = useState(initial?.in_chapa ?? true);
  const [accessEmail, setAccessEmail] = useState(linkedEmail ?? "");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const creatingLogin = isOwner && !initial;

  const linkExisting = async (email: string, memberId: string, nextPassword: string) => {
    const match = accounts.find((account) => account.email.toLowerCase() === email);
    if (!match) return "JÃ¡ existe uma conta com esse e-mail.";
    if (match.is_owner) return "Esta conta Ã© do responsÃ¡vel pela plataforma.";
    const taken = members.find((member) => member.user_id === match.user_id && member.id !== memberId);
    if (taken) return `Este e-mail jÃ¡ Ã© o acesso de ${taken.name}.`;
    const { error: linkError } = await supabase.from("board_members").update({ user_id: match.user_id }).eq("id", memberId);
    if (linkError) return "NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel vincular o acesso.";
    if (nextPassword) {
      const updated = await callAdminUsers({ action: "set_password", user_id: match.user_id, password: nextPassword });
      if (updated.error) return updated.error;
    }
    return null;
  };

  const ensureAccess = async (memberId: string, userId: string | null) => {
    if (!isOwner) return { error: null, notice: null as string | null };
    const email = accessEmail.trim().toLowerCase();
    const nextPassword = password.trim();
    if (!userId && !email && !nextPassword) return initial ? { error: null, notice: null } : { error: "Informe o e-mail e a senha do acesso deste membro.", notice: null };
    if (!userId && (!email || nextPassword.length < 8)) return { error: "Informe o e-mail e uma senha de pelo menos 8 caracteres.", notice: null };
    if (userId && !email) return { error: "Informe o e-mail de acesso.", notice: null };

    if (!userId) {
      const created = await callAdminUsers({ action: "create", email, password: nextPassword });
      if (created.code === "email_exists") {
        const linked = await linkExisting(email, memberId, nextPassword);
        return linked ? { error: linked, notice: null } : { error: null, notice: `Acesso de ${email} vinculado a ${name.trim()}.` };
      }
      if (created.error || !created.userId) return { error: created.error ?? "NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel criar o acesso.", notice: null };
      const { error: linkError } = await supabase.from("board_members").update({ user_id: created.userId }).eq("id", memberId);
      if (linkError) {
        await callAdminUsers({ action: "delete", user_id: created.userId });
        return { error: "NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel vincular o acesso ao membro.", notice: null };
      }
      return { error: null, notice: `Acesso criado para ${name.trim()}. E-mail ${email} Â· senha ${nextPassword}. PeÃ§a para trocar a senha no primeiro acesso.` };
    }

    const emailChanged = email !== (linkedEmail ?? "").toLowerCase();
    if (emailChanged) {
      const updated = await callAdminUsers({ action: "set_email", user_id: userId, email });
      if (updated.error) return { error: updated.error, notice: null };
    }
    if (nextPassword) {
      if (nextPassword.length < 8) return { error: "A senha precisa ter pelo menos 8 caracteres.", notice: null };
      const updated = await callAdminUsers({ action: "set_password", user_id: userId, password: nextPassword });
      if (updated.error) return { error: updated.error, notice: null };
      return { error: null, notice: `Senha de ${name.trim()} alterada para ${nextPassword}.` };
    }
    return { error: null, notice: emailChanged ? `E-mail de acesso de ${name.trim()} alterado para ${email}.` : null };
  };

  const save = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const handle = cleanInstagram(instagram);
    if (handle && !/^[A-Za-z0-9._]{1,30}$/.test(handle)) return setError("Instagram invÃ¡lido. Use sÃ³ o usuÃ¡rio, ex.: da_direito.");
    setBusy(true); setError("");
    const payload = { name: name.trim(), role: role.trim(), sector: sector.trim(), instagram: handle || null, photo_url: photo, active, in_chapa: inChapa };
    if (!initial) {
      const inserted = await supabase.from("board_members").insert({ ...payload, sort_order: nextOrder }).select("id").single();
      if (inserted.error || !inserted.data) { setBusy(false); return setError("NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel salvar o membro."); }
      const access = await ensureAccess(inserted.data.id, null);
      if (access.error) {
        await supabase.from("board_members").delete().eq("id", inserted.data.id);
        setBusy(false);
        return setError(access.error);
      }
      setBusy(false);
      return onDone(access.notice ?? undefined);
    }
    const { error: dbError } = await supabase.from("board_members").update(payload).eq("id", initial.id);
    if (dbError) { setBusy(false); return setError("NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel salvar o membro."); }
    const access = await ensureAccess(initial.id, initial.user_id);
    setBusy(false);
    if (access.error) return setError(access.error);
    onDone(access.notice ?? undefined);
  };

  const removeAccess = async () => {
    if (!initial?.user_id) return;
    if (!window.confirm(`Remover o acesso de ${linkedEmail ?? name}? A pessoa deixa de entrar no painel. O cadastro do membro continua.`)) return;
    setBusy(true); setError("");
    const failure = await callAdminUsers({ action: "delete", user_id: initial.user_id });
    setBusy(false);
    if (failure.error) return setError(failure.error);
    onDone(`Acesso de ${name.trim()} removido. O cadastro continua na pÃ¡gina.`);
  };

  return <form className="grid gap-4 border-b border-border bg-muted/50 p-5 sm:grid-cols-2" onSubmit={save}>
    <Field label="Nome"><input required minLength={2} maxLength={120} value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} placeholder="Helena Duarte" className={inputClass} /></Field>
    <Field label="Cargo"><input required minLength={2} maxLength={80} value={role} onChange={(e) => setRole(e.target.value)} placeholder="Presidente" className={inputClass} /></Field>
    <Field label="Setor" hint="membros do mesmo setor aparecem juntos no site">
      <input required minLength={2} maxLength={60} list="member-sectors" value={sector} onChange={(e) => setSector(e.target.value)} placeholder="Diretoria" className={inputClass} />
      <datalist id="member-sectors">{sectors.map((s) => <option key={s} value={s} />)}</datalist>
    </Field>
    <Field label="Instagram" hint="opcional"><input value={instagram} onChange={(e) => setInstagram(e.target.value)} placeholder="@usuario" className={inputClass} /></Field>
    <ImageInput label="Foto" value={photo} onChange={setPhoto} folder="board" />
    <div className="grid content-start gap-2.5 pt-1">
      <Toggle label="Faz parte da chapa" checked={inChapa} onChange={setInChapa} />
      <Toggle label="Exibir no site" checked={active} onChange={setActive} />
      <p className="text-xs font-normal text-muted-foreground">Quem nÃ£o faz parte da chapa pode ser ocultado ou removido sem alterar os demais.</p>
    </div>
    {isOwner ? <>
      <Field label="E-mail de acesso" hint="login prÃ³prio deste membro no painel"><input required={creatingLogin} type="email" value={accessEmail} onChange={(e) => setAccessEmail(e.target.value)} placeholder="email@exemplo.com" className={inputClass} /></Field>
      <Field label={initial?.user_id ? "Nova senha" : "Senha inicial"} hint={initial?.user_id ? "em branco, mantÃ©m a senha atual" : "mÃ­nimo de 8 caracteres"}>
        <div className="flex gap-2"><input required={creatingLogin} minLength={creatingLogin ? 8 : undefined} value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} className={`${inputClass} font-mono`} autoComplete="new-password" /><Button type="button" variant="outline" size="icon" className="size-11 shrink-0" onClick={() => setPassword(generatePassword())} aria-label="Gerar senha"><Wand2 size={16} /></Button></div>
      </Field>
    </> : <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground sm:col-span-2">{initial?.user_id ? `Acesso ao painel: ${linkedEmail ?? "conta vinculada"}` : "Sem acesso ao painel. SÃ³ o responsÃ¡vel cria o login."}</p>}
    <ErrorNote text={error} />
    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 sm:col-span-2">
      <Button type="submit" disabled={busy}>{busy ? "Salvando..." : "Salvar"}</Button>
      {isOwner && initial?.user_id && <Button type="button" variant="ghost" className="text-destructive" disabled={busy} onClick={removeAccess}>Remover acesso</Button>}
      <Button type="button" variant="ghost" onClick={onCancel}>Cancelar</Button>
    </div>
  </form>;
}


==================== src\components\admin\orders-admin.tsx ====================
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useState } from "react";
import { Constants } from "@/integrations/supabase/types";
import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
import { money } from "@/lib/catalog";
import { formatDateTime } from "@/lib/data";
import { type OrderStatus, orderStatusBadge, orderStatusLabel } from "@/lib/orders";
import { Panel } from "./form";

const statuses = Constants.public.Enums.order_status;

export function OrdersAdmin() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<OrderStatus | "todos">("todos");
  const { data: orders = [], isLoading } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["admin", "orders"],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.from("orders").select("*, order_items(*)").order("created_at", { ascending: false });
      if (error) throw error;
      return data;
    },
  });
  const visible = orders.filter((o) => filter === "todos" || o.status === filter);
  const setStatus = async (id: string, status: OrderStatus) => {
    await supabase.from("orders").update({ status }).eq("id", id);
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin"] });
  };

  return <Panel title="Pedidos" subtitle="Acompanhe a produÃ§Ã£o e dÃª baixa nas retiradas presenciais.">
    <p className="mx-5 mt-4 rounded-lg border border-gold/40 bg-background p-3 text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground">A loja online ainda nÃ£o registra pedidos. Esta lista jÃ¡ estÃ¡ ligada ao banco e passa a ser preenchida quando as vendas forem ativadas.</p>
    <div className="-mx-1 flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto px-6 py-4">
      {(["todos", ...statuses] as const).map((s) => <button key={s} onClick={() => setFilter(s)} className={`shrink-0 rounded-full border px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold ${filter === s ? "border-primary bg-primary text-primary-foreground" : "border-border bg-background text-muted-foreground"}`}>{s === "todos" ? "Todos" : orderStatusLabel[s]}</button>)}
    </div>
    <div className="overflow-x-auto">
      <table className="w-full min-w-[760px] text-left text-sm">
        <thead className="bg-muted text-xs uppercase text-muted-foreground"><tr><th className="px-5 py-3">Pedido</th><th className="px-5 py-3">Aluno</th><th className="px-5 py-3">Itens</th><th className="px-5 py-3">Total</th><th className="px-5 py-3">Status</th></tr></thead>
        <tbody>
          {isLoading && <tr><td colSpan={5} className="px-5 py-8"><div className="h-8 animate-pulse rounded bg-muted" /></td></tr>}
          {visible.map((o) => <tr key={o.id} className="border-t border-border">
            <td className="px-5 py-4 font-bold">{o.code}<p className="text-xs font-normal text-muted-foreground">{formatDateTime(o.created_at)}</p></td>
            <td className="px-5 py-4 font-semibold">{o.full_name}<p className="text-xs font-normal text-muted-foreground">{o.email}</p></td>
            <td className="px-5 py-4 text-muted-foreground">{o.order_items.map((it) => `${it.quantity}Ã— ${it.product_name}${it.size ? ` Â· ${it.size}` : ""}`).join(", ")}</td>
            <td className="px-5 py-4 font-bold">{money(Number(o.total))}</td>
            <td className="px-5 py-4">
              <select value={o.status} onChange={(e) => setStatus(o.id, e.target.value as OrderStatus)} className={`rounded-full border-0 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-bold outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-ring ${orderStatusBadge[o.status]}`}>
                {statuses.map((s) => <option key={s} value={s}>{orderStatusLabel[s]}</option>)}
              </select>
            </td>
          </tr>)}
          {!isLoading && visible.length === 0 && <tr><td colSpan={5} className="px-5 py-8 text-center text-muted-foreground">Nenhum pedido{filter === "todos" ? "" : " com este status"}.</td></tr>}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  </Panel>;
}


==================== src\components\admin\pages-admin.tsx ====================
import { useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useState } from "react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
import { type PageKey, pageDefaults, pageFields, usePageContent } from "@/lib/pages";
import { ErrorNote, Field, ImageInput, Panel, inputClass } from "./form";

export function PagesAdmin() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const { data } = usePageContent();
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<Record<PageKey, string> | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false);
  const current = draft ?? data ?? pageDefaults;
  const groups = [...new Set(pageFields.map((field) => field.group))];

  const set = (key: PageKey, value: string) => {
    setSaved(false);
    setDraft({ ...current, [key]: value });
  };

  const save = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setBusy(true); setError(""); setSaved(false);
    const entries = pageFields.map((field) => ({ key: field.key, value: current[field.key].trim() }));
    const { error: rpcError } = await supabase.rpc("save_page_content", { _entries: entries });
    setBusy(false);
    if (rpcError) return setError("NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel salvar os textos.");
    setDraft(null);
    setSaved(true);
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["page-content"] });
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin", "activity"] });
  };

  return <Panel title="PÃ¡ginas" subtitle="Textos, o logo e a imagem padrÃ£o do site. Cores e disposiÃ§Ã£o das pÃ¡ginas continuam iguais.">
    <form onSubmit={save} className="grid gap-8 p-5">
      {groups.map((group) => <fieldset key={group} className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
        <legend className="mb-1 text-sm font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-gold">{group}</legend>
        {pageFields.filter((field) => field.group === group).map((field) => "image" in field && field.image
          ? <div key={field.key} className="sm:col-span-2"><ImageInput label={field.label} folder="pages" value={current[field.key] || null} onChange={(url) => set(field.key, url ?? "")} />{"hint" in field && field.hint && <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{field.hint}</p>}</div>
          : <Field key={field.key} label={field.label} className={field.long ? "sm:col-span-2" : ""}>
            {field.long
              ? <textarea rows={3} maxLength={800} value={current[field.key]} onChange={(e) => set(field.key, e.target.value)} className={`${inputClass} h-auto py-2`} />
              : <input maxLength={120} value={current[field.key]} onChange={(e) => set(field.key, e.target.value)} className={inputClass} />}
          </Field>)}
      </fieldset>)}
      <ErrorNote text={error} />
      {saved && <p className="rounded-lg bg-success-soft p-3 text-xs font-bold text-success">Textos atualizados. A pÃ¡gina pÃºblica jÃ¡ usa a versÃ£o nova.</p>}
      <div><Button type="submit" disabled={busy}>{busy ? "Salvando..." : "Salvar pÃ¡ginas"}</Button></div>
    </form>
  </Panel>;
}


==================== src\components\admin\products-admin.tsx ====================
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { EyeOff, Package, Pencil, Plus, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import { useState } from "react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
import { money } from "@/lib/catalog";
import { type ProductRow, type ProductStatus, productStatusLabel } from "@/lib/data";
import { ErrorNote, Field, ImageInput, Panel, Toggle, inputClass } from "./form";

export function ProductsAdmin() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const { data: items = [], isLoading } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["admin", "products"],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.from("products").select("*").order("sort_order").order("created_at");
      if (error) throw error;
      return data;
    },
  });
  const [form, setForm] = useState<{ mode: "new" } | { mode: "edit"; item: ProductRow } | null>(null);
  const refresh = () => { queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin"] }); queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["products"] }); };
  const remove = async (p: ProductRow) => {
    if (!window.confirm(`Excluir "${p.name}"?`)) return;
    await supabase.from("products").delete().eq("id", p.id);
    refresh();
  };

  return <Panel title="CatÃ¡logo" subtitle="Crie, edite, oculte ou exclua produtos da loja." action={<Button onClick={() => setForm({ mode: "new" })}><Plus /> Novo produto</Button>}>
    {form && <ProductForm key={form.mode === "edit" ? form.item.id : "new"} initial={form.mode === "edit" ? form.item : null} nextOrder={items.length + 1} onDone={() => { setForm(null); refresh(); }} onCancel={() => setForm(null)} />}
    <ul className="divide-y divide-border">
      {isLoading && <li className="p-6"><div className="h-12 animate-pulse rounded-lg bg-muted" /></li>}
      {items.map((p) => <li key={p.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 p-4 sm:gap-4 sm:px-5">
        {p.image_url ? <img src={p.image_url} alt="" width={96} height={96} className={`size-12 shrink-0 rounded-lg object-cover grayscale ${p.active ? "" : "opacity-50"}`} /> : <span className="grid size-12 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-lg bg-muted text-muted-foreground"><Package size={20} /></span>}
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1"><p className="flex items-center gap-1.5 truncate font-bold">{!p.active && <EyeOff size={14} className="shrink-0 text-muted-foreground" />}{p.name}</p><p className="truncate text-xs text-muted-foreground">{money(Number(p.price))}{p.sizes.length ? ` Â· ${p.sizes.join(" Â· ")}` : ""}{p.status !== "disponivel" && ` Â· ${productStatusLabel[p.status]}`}{!p.active && " Â· Oculto"}</p></div>
        <Button variant="outline" size="icon" onClick={() => setForm({ mode: "edit", item: p })} aria-label={`Editar ${p.name}`}><Pencil size={16} /></Button>
        <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" className="text-destructive" onClick={() => remove(p)} aria-label={`Excluir ${p.name}`}><Trash2 size={16} /></Button>
      </li>)}
      {!isLoading && items.length === 0 && <li className="p-6 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">Nenhum produto cadastrado.</li>}
    </ul>
  </Panel>;
}
