==================== src\components\app-shell.tsx ====================
import { Link, useRouterState } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { CalendarDays, Home, Package, Scale, ShieldCheck } from "lucide-react";
import { useEffect, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { pageText, usePageContent } from "@/lib/pages";

const nav = [
  { to: "/", label: "InÃ­cio", icon: Home },
  { to: "/produtos", label: "Produtos", icon: Package },
  { to: "/eventos", label: "Eventos", icon: CalendarDays },
  { to: "/admin", label: "Diretoria", icon: ShieldCheck },
] as const;

export function BrandMark({ className = "size-10", icon = 23 }: { className?: string; icon?: number }) {
  const { data: pages, isPending } = usePageContent();
  if (isPending || !pages) return <span className={`block ${className} shrink-0 rounded-lg bg-primary`} aria-hidden="true" />;
  const logo = pageText(pages, "site_logo");
  if (logo) return <img src={logo} alt="" className={`block ${className} shrink-0 rounded-lg bg-primary object-cover`} />;
  return <span className={`grid ${className} shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-lg bg-primary text-gold`}><Scale size={icon} /></span>;
}

export function AppShell({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const pathname = useRouterState({ select: (state) => state.location.pathname });
  const { data: pages } = usePageContent();
  const siteName = pageText(pages, "site_name");
  const logo = pageText(pages, "site_logo");
  useEffect(() => {
    const link = document.querySelector<HTMLLinkElement>('link[rel="icon"]');
    if (!link) return;
    link.href = logo || "/favicon.svg";
    link.type = logo ? "" : "image/svg+xml";
  }, [logo]);
  return <div className="min-h-screen bg-background pb-20 md:pb-0">
    <header className="sticky top-0 z-40 border-b border-border bg-card/95 backdrop-blur">
      <div className="app-container grid h-16 grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_auto] items-center gap-4">
        <Link to="/" className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-3" aria-label={`${siteName} â€” InÃ­cio`}>
          <BrandMark />
          <span className="min-w-0"><strong className="block truncate font-display text-base">{siteName}</strong><small className="block truncate text-[10px] font-bold uppercase text-muted-foreground">{pageText(pages, "site_tagline")}</small></span>
        </Link>
        <nav className="hidden items-center gap-1 md:flex" aria-label="NavegaÃ§Ã£o principal">
          {nav.slice(0, -1).map(({ to, label }) => <Link key={to} to={to} className="rounded-md px-4 py-2 text-sm font-bold text-muted-foreground hover:bg-muted hover:text-foreground" activeProps={{ className: "bg-navy-soft text-primary" }}>{label}</Link>)}
          <Link to="/admin" className="ml-2 rounded-md border border-border px-4 py-2 text-sm font-bold" activeProps={{ className: "border-primary bg-navy-soft text-primary" }}>Diretoria</Link>
        </nav>
      </div>
    </header>
    <main>{children}</main>
    <nav className="fixed inset-x-0 bottom-0 z-50 grid h-[72px] grid-cols-4 border-t border-border bg-card px-2 pb-[env(safe-area-inset-bottom)] md:hidden" aria-label="NavegaÃ§Ã£o inferior">
      {nav.map(({ to, label, icon: Icon }) => { const active = to === "/" ? pathname === "/" : pathname.startsWith(to); return <Link key={to} to={to} className={`flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-bold ${active ? "text-primary" : "text-muted-foreground"}`}><Icon size={21} strokeWidth={active ? 2.5 : 2} /><span>{label}</span>{active && <span className="absolute bottom-1 h-1 w-1 rounded-full bg-gold" />}</Link>; })}
    </nav>
  </div>;
}

==================== src\components\event-gallery.tsx ====================
import { Download, X } from "lucide-react";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { slugify, useEventPhotos } from "@/lib/data";

function fileName(title: string, url: string, index: number) {
  const ext = url.split(".").pop()?.split("?")[0]?.toLowerCase() ?? "jpg";
  const safe = ext === "jpeg" || ext === "jpg" || ext === "png" || ext === "webp" ? (ext === "jpeg" ? "jpg" : ext) : "jpg";
  return `${slugify(title) || "evento"}-${String(index + 1).padStart(2, "0")}.${safe}`;
}

async function downloadPhoto(url: string, name: string) {
  const response = await fetch(url);
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error("download");
  const blob = await response.blob();
  const objectUrl = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const link = Object.assign(document.createElement("a"), { href: objectUrl, download: name });
  document.body.appendChild(link);
  link.click();
  link.remove();
  URL.revokeObjectURL(objectUrl);
}

export function EventGallery({ eventId, title }: { eventId: string; title: string }) {
  const { data: photos = [], isLoading } = useEventPhotos(eventId);
  const [open, setOpen] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const current = open != null ? photos[open] : undefined;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (open == null) return;
    const onKey = (event: KeyboardEvent) => { if (event.key === "Escape") setOpen(null); };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  }, [open]);

  const save = async (index: number) => {
    const photo = photos[index];
    if (!photo) return;
    setError("");
    try { await downloadPhoto(photo.image_url, fileName(title, photo.image_url, index)); }
    catch { setError("NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel baixar esta foto. Tente de novo."); }
  };

  if (isLoading) return <div className="mt-7 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-4">{[0, 1, 2, 3].map((i) => <div key={i} className="aspect-square animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-muted" />)}</div>;
  if (photos.length === 0) return <p className="mt-7 rounded-lg border border-border bg-card p-6 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">As fotos deste evento ainda nÃ£o foram publicadas.</p>;

  return <div className="mt-7">
    <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{photos.length} {photos.length === 1 ? "foto" : "fotos"}. Clique para ampliar e baixar.</p>
    {error && <p className="mt-3 rounded-lg bg-destructive/10 p-3 text-xs font-bold text-destructive">{error}</p>}
    <div className="mt-4 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-4">
      {photos.map((photo, index) => <div key={photo.id} className="group relative overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-border bg-card">
        <button type="button" className="block w-full" onClick={() => setOpen(index)} aria-label={`Ampliar foto ${index + 1}`}>
          <img src={photo.image_url} alt="" loading="lazy" className="aspect-square w-full object-cover" />
        </button>
        <button type="button" className="absolute bottom-2 right-2 grid size-9 place-items-center rounded-lg bg-primary/90 text-gold" onClick={() => save(index)} aria-label={`Baixar foto ${index + 1}`}><Download size={16} /></button>
      </div>)}
    </div>
    {current && open != null && <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/80 p-4" onClick={() => setOpen(null)}>
      <div className="flex max-h-full max-w-5xl flex-col items-center gap-4" onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}>
        <img src={current.image_url} alt="" className="max-h-[75vh] max-w-full rounded-lg object-contain" />
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <Button onClick={() => save(open)}><Download /> Baixar</Button>
          <Button variant="outline" className="bg-card" onClick={() => setOpen(null)}><X /> Fechar</Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>}
  </div>;
}


==================== src\components\product-card.tsx ====================
import { Link } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { Package } from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { money } from "@/lib/catalog";
import { type ProductRow, productStatusLabel } from "@/lib/data";

export function ProductCard({ product }: { product: Pick<ProductRow, "id" | "name" | "price" | "image_url" | "sizes" | "status"> }) {
  const available = product.status === "disponivel";
  return <article className="min-w-0 overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-border bg-card shadow-sm">
    <div className="relative">
      {product.image_url
        ? <img src={product.image_url} alt={product.name} loading="lazy" width={912} height={912} className={`aspect-square w-full object-cover grayscale ${available ? "" : "opacity-60"}`} />
        : <div className="grid aspect-square w-full place-items-center bg-muted text-muted-foreground"><Package size={40} /></div>}
      {!available && <span className={`absolute left-3 top-3 rounded-full px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-extrabold uppercase ${product.status === "esgotado" ? "bg-primary text-primary-foreground" : "bg-gold text-gold-foreground"}`}>{productStatusLabel[product.status]}</span>}
    </div>
    <div className="p-4"><h3 className="text-base font-bold">{product.name}</h3><p className="mt-1 text-lg font-extrabold text-primary">{money(Number(product.price))}</p>
      {product.sizes.length > 0 && <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5" aria-label="Tamanhos disponÃ­veis">{product.sizes.map((size) => <span key={size} className="rounded-md border border-border bg-muted px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-bold">{size}</span>)}</div>}
      {available
        ? <Button asChild variant="gold" className="mt-4 h-11 w-full"><Link to="/checkout" search={{ item: product.id }}>Garantir no Lote Atual</Link></Button>
        : <Button variant="outline" className="mt-4 h-11 w-full" disabled>{product.status === "esgotado" ? "Esgotado" : "DisponÃ­vel em breve"}</Button>}
    </div>
  </article>;
}

export function ProductCardSkeleton() {
  return <div className="min-w-0 overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-border bg-card"><div className="aspect-square w-full animate-pulse bg-muted" /><div className="space-y-3 p-4"><div className="h-4 w-2/3 animate-pulse rounded bg-muted" /><div className="h-5 w-1/3 animate-pulse rounded bg-muted" /><div className="h-11 animate-pulse rounded-lg bg-muted" /></div></div>;
}


==================== src\components\qr-code.tsx ====================
export function FakeQrCode({ seed = 1, size = "md" }: { seed?: number; size?: "sm" | "md" }) {
  const cells = Array.from({ length: 121 }, (_, i) => ((i * 17 + seed * 13) % 11 < 5) || i % 19 === 0);
  return <div className={`grid grid-cols-11 gap-px rounded-md bg-card p-2 shadow-sm ${size === "sm" ? "size-24" : "size-44"}`} aria-label="QR Code simulado">
    {cells.map((on, index) => <span key={index} className={on ? "bg-primary" : "bg-card"} />)}
  </div>;
}

==================== src\hooks\use-mobile.tsx ====================
import * as React from "react";

const MOBILE_BREAKPOINT = 768;

export function useIsMobile() {
  const [isMobile, setIsMobile] = React.useState<boolean | undefined>(undefined);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    const mql = window.matchMedia(`(max-width: ${MOBILE_BREAKPOINT - 1}px)`);
    const onChange = () => {
      setIsMobile(window.innerWidth < MOBILE_BREAKPOINT);
    };
    mql.addEventListener("change", onChange);
    setIsMobile(window.innerWidth < MOBILE_BREAKPOINT);
    return () => mql.removeEventListener("change", onChange);
  }, []);

  return !!isMobile;
}

==================== src\integrations\supabase\auth-attacher.ts ====================
// This file is automatically generated. Do not edit it directly.
import { createMiddleware } from '@tanstack/react-start'
import { supabase } from './client'

// Must be registered as a global `functionMiddleware` in `src/start.ts`; otherwise
// the browser never attaches the bearer token to serverFn RPCs.
export const attachSupabaseAuth = createMiddleware({ type: 'function' }).client(
  async ({ next }) => {
    const { data } = await supabase.auth.getSession()
    const token = data.session?.access_token
    return next({
      headers: token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {},
    })
  },
)


==================== src\integrations\supabase\auth-middleware.ts ====================
// This file is automatically generated. Do not edit it directly.
import { createMiddleware } from '@tanstack/react-start'
import { getRequest } from '@tanstack/react-start/server'
import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'
import type { Database } from './types'




function isNewSupabaseApiKey(value: string): boolean {
  return value.startsWith('sb_publishable_') || value.startsWith('sb_secret_');
}

function createSupabaseFetch(supabaseKey: string): typeof fetch {
  return (input, init) => {
    const headers = new Headers(
      typeof Request !== 'undefined' && input instanceof Request ? input.headers : undefined,
    );

    if (init?.headers) {
      new Headers(init.headers).forEach((value, key) => headers.set(key, value));
    }

    // New Supabase API keys are opaque strings, not bearer JWTs.
    if (isNewSupabaseApiKey(supabaseKey) && headers.get('Authorization') === `Bearer ${supabaseKey}`) {
      headers.delete('Authorization');
    }

    headers.set('apikey', supabaseKey);
    return fetch(input, { ...init, headers });
  };
}

export const requireSupabaseAuth = createMiddleware({ type: 'function' }).server(
  async ({ next }) => {
    
    const SUPABASE_URL = process.env['SUPABASE_URL'];
    const SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY = process.env['SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY'];

    if (!SUPABASE_URL || !SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY) {
      const missing = [
        ...(!SUPABASE_URL ? ['SUPABASE_URL'] : []),
        ...(!SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY ? ['SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY'] : []),
      ];
      const message = `Missing Supabase environment variable(s): ${missing.join(', ')}. Configure as variÃ¡veis do Supabase no arquivo .env ou na hospedagem.`;
      console.error(`[Supabase] ${message}`);
      throw new Error(message);
    }
    
    const request = getRequest();

    if (!request?.headers) {
      throw new Error('Unauthorized: No request headers available');
    }


    const authHeader = request.headers.get('authorization');

    if (!authHeader) {
      throw new Error('Unauthorized: No authorization header provided');
    }

    if (!authHeader.startsWith('Bearer ')) {
      throw new Error('Unauthorized: Only Bearer tokens are supported');
    }

    const token = authHeader.replace('Bearer ', '');
    if (!token) {
      throw new Error('Unauthorized: No token provided');
    }

    if (token.split('.').length !== 3) {
      throw new Error('Unauthorized: Invalid token');
    }

    const supabase = createClient<Database>(
      SUPABASE_URL!,
      SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY!,
      {
        global: {
          fetch: createSupabaseFetch(SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY!),
          headers: {
            Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
          },
        },
        auth: {
          storage: undefined,
          persistSession: false,
          autoRefreshToken: false,
        },
      }
    );

    const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.getClaims(token);
    if (error || !data?.claims) {
      throw new Error('Unauthorized: Invalid token');
    }

    if (!data.claims.sub) {
      throw new Error('Unauthorized: No user ID found in token');
    }

    return next({
      context: {
        supabase,
        userId: data.claims.sub,
        claims: data.claims,
      },
    });
  },
);

import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
import type { Database } from './types';

function isNewSupabaseApiKey(value: string): boolean {
  return value.startsWith('sb_publishable_') || value.startsWith('sb_secret_');
}

function createSupabaseFetch(supabaseKey: string): typeof fetch {
  return (input, init) => {
    const headers = new Headers(
      typeof Request !== 'undefined' && input instanceof Request ? input.headers : undefined,
    );

    if (init?.headers) {
      new Headers(init.headers).forEach((value, key) => headers.set(key, value));
    }

    // New Supabase API keys are opaque strings, not bearer JWTs.
    if (isNewSupabaseApiKey(supabaseKey) && headers.get('Authorization') === `Bearer ${supabaseKey}`) {
      headers.delete('Authorization');
    }

    headers.set('apikey', supabaseKey);
    return fetch(input, { ...init, headers });
  };

function createSupabaseClient() {
  // Use import.meta.env for client-side (Vite build-time replacement)
  // Fall back to process.env for SSR (server-side rendering)
  const SUPABASE_URL = import.meta.env['VITE_SUPABASE_URL'] || process.env['SUPABASE_URL'];
  const SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY = import.meta.env['VITE_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY'] || process.env['SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY'];

  if (!SUPABASE_URL || !SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY) {
    const missing = [
      ...(!SUPABASE_URL ? ['SUPABASE_URL'] : []),
      ...(!SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY ? ['SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY'] : []),
    ];
    const message = `Missing Supabase environment variable(s): ${missing.join(', ')}. Configure as variÃ¡veis do Supabase no arquivo .env ou na hospedagem.`;
    console.error(`[Supabase] ${message}`);
    throw new Error(message);
  }

  return createClient<Database>(SUPABASE_URL, SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY, {
    global: {
      fetch: createSupabaseFetch(SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY),
    },
    auth: {
      storage: typeof window === 'undefined' ? undefined : window.localStorage,
      persistSession: true,
      autoRefreshToken: true,
    },
  });
}

let _supabase: ReturnType<typeof createSupabaseClient> | undefined;

// Import the supabase client like this:
// import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
export const supabase = new Proxy({} as ReturnType<typeof createSupabaseClient>, {
  get(_, prop, receiver) {
    if (!_supabase) _supabase = createSupabaseClient();
    return Reflect.get(_supabase, prop, receiver);
  },
});

==================== src\lib\auth.ts ====================
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useNavigate } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";

export function safeRedirect(value: unknown): string {
  return typeof value === "string" && value.startsWith("/") && !value.startsWith("//") ? value : "/admin";
}

/** Current user + roles, re-validated with the auth server. */
export function useCurrentUser() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ["current-user"],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const { data } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
      const user = data.user;
      if (!user) return null;
      const { data: roles } = await supabase.from("user_roles").select("role").eq("user_id", user.id);
      const list = (roles ?? []).map((r) => r.role);
      return { user, isAdmin: list.includes("admin"), isOwner: list.includes("owner") };
    },
    staleTime: 30_000,
  });
}

export function useSignOut() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  return async () => {
    await queryClient.cancelQueries();
    queryClient.clear();
    await supabase.auth.signOut();
    navigate({ to: "/auth", replace: true });
  };
}

==================== src\lib\certificate.ts ====================
import { format } from "date-fns";
import { ptBR } from "date-fns/locale";
import { formatEventDays, slugify } from "@/lib/data";

export type CertificateSigner = {
  role: string;
  name: string;
  signature_url: string | null;
};

export type CertificateTemplateFields = {
  da_name: string | null;
  da_signature: string | null;
  coordinator_name: string | null;
  coordinator_signature: string | null;
  athletics_name: string | null;
  athletics_signature: string | null;
};

export type CertificateData = {
  name: string;
  ra: string;
  event_title: string;
  starts_at: string;
  ends_at: string | null;
  location: string | null;
  hours: number | null;
  hour_type: "complementar" | "obrigatoria";
  signers: CertificateSigner[];
};

const INK: [number, number, number] = [28, 28, 28];
const GOLD: [number, number, number] = [184, 150, 60];
const MUTED: [number, number, number] = [110, 110, 110];

const hoursText = (h: number) => `${String(h).replace(".", ",")} hora${h === 1 ? "" : "s"}`;

export const certificateRoles = [
  { role: "Presidente do DA", name: "da_name", signature: "da_signature" },
  { role: "Coordenador", name: "coordinator_name", signature: "coordinator_signature" },
  { role: "Presidente da AtlÃ©tica", name: "athletics_name", signature: "athletics_signature" },
] as const;

export function templateSigners(template: CertificateTemplateFields): CertificateSigner[] {
  return certificateRoles.map((slot) => ({
    role: slot.role,
    name: template[slot.name]?.trim() ?? "",
    signature_url: template[slot.signature],
  }));
}

export function templateReady(template: CertificateTemplateFields | null | undefined) {
  if (!template) return false;
  return templateSigners(template).every((signer) => signer.name.length > 0 && !!signer.signature_url);
}

export function readSigners(value: unknown): CertificateSigner[] | null {
  if (!Array.isArray(value) || value.length !== 3) return null;
  const signers: CertificateSigner[] = [];
  for (const item of value) {
    if (!item || typeof item !== "object") return null;
    const row = item as Record<string, unknown>;
    const role = row["role"];
    const name = row["name"];
    const signature = row["signature_url"];
    if (typeof role !== "string" || typeof name !== "string" || typeof signature !== "string") return null;
    if (!role || !name.trim() || !signature) return null;
    signers.push({ role, name: name.trim(), signature_url: signature });
  }
  return signers;
}

/** Sample lines so the board can check the layout before a student downloads. */
export function blankCertificate(signers: CertificateSigner[]): CertificateData {
  return {
    name: "Nome do aluno",
    ra: "0000000",
    event_title: "Nome do evento",
    starts_at: "2026-11-03T15:00:00.000Z",
    ends_at: "2026-11-06T15:00:00.000Z",
    location: "Local do evento",
    hours: 10,
    hour_type: "complementar",
    signers,
  };
}

async function loadSignature(url: string) {
  const response = await fetch(url);
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error("signature");
  const bitmap = await createImageBitmap(await response.blob());
  const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
  canvas.width = bitmap.width;
  canvas.height = bitmap.height;
  const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
  if (!ctx) throw new Error("signature");
  ctx.fillStyle = "#ffffff";
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
  ctx.drawImage(bitmap, 0, 0);
  bitmap.close();
  return canvas.toDataURL("image/png");
}

/** Builds the A4 landscape certificate in the browser and starts the download. */
export async function downloadCertificate(c: CertificateData) {
  const { jsPDF } = await import("jspdf");
  const doc = new jsPDF({ orientation: "landscape", unit: "mm", format: "a4" });
  const W = 297;
  const H = 210;
  const cx = W / 2;
  const images = await Promise.all(c.signers.map((signer) => (signer.signature_url ? loadSignature(signer.signature_url) : Promise.resolve(null))));

  doc.setFillColor(...INK);
  doc.rect(0, 0, W, H, "F");
  doc.setFillColor(255, 255, 255);
  doc.rect(10, 10, W - 20, H - 20, "F");
  doc.setDrawColor(...GOLD);
  doc.setLineWidth(0.8);
  doc.rect(15, 15, W - 30, H - 30);
  doc.setLineWidth(0.3);
  doc.rect(17.5, 17.5, W - 35, H - 35);

  const spaced = (text: string, y: number, charSpace: number) => {
    const width = doc.getTextWidth(text) + charSpace * (text.length - 1);
    doc.text(text, cx - width / 2, y, { charSpace });
  };
  const fit = (text: string, width: number, size: number, min: number) => {
    let next = size;
    doc.setFontSize(next);
    while (next > min && doc.getTextWidth(text) > width) {
      next -= 0.5;
      doc.setFontSize(next);
    }
  };

  doc.setTextColor(...GOLD);
  doc.setFont("helvetica", "bold");
  doc.setFontSize(11);
  spaced("DIRETÃ“RIO ACADÃŠMICO DE DIREITO", 36, 1.5);

  doc.setTextColor(...INK);
  doc.setFont("times", "bold");
  doc.setFontSize(36);
  spaced("CERTIFICADO", 54, 2);
  doc.setDrawColor(...GOLD);
  doc.setLineWidth(0.6);
  doc.line(cx - 28, 59, cx + 28, 59);

  doc.setFont("helvetica", "normal");
  doc.setFontSize(12);
  doc.setTextColor(...MUTED);
  doc.text("Certificamos que", cx, 74, { align: "center" });

  doc.setFont("times", "bold");
  doc.setTextColor(...INK);
  fit(c.name, W - 70, 26, 14);
  doc.text(c.name, cx, 88, { align: "center" });

  const when = formatEventDays(c);
  const year = format(new Date(c.starts_at), "yyyy");
  const parts = [
    `participou do evento â€œ${c.event_title}â€, realizado em ${when} de ${year}`,
    c.location ? `, em ${c.location}` : "",
    c.hours ? `, com carga horÃ¡ria de ${hoursText(c.hours)} ${c.hour_type === "obrigatoria" ? "obrigatÃ³rias" : "complementares"}.` : ".",
  ];
  doc.setFont("helvetica", "normal");
  doc.setFontSize(12);
  doc.setTextColor(...INK);
  doc.text(doc.splitTextToSize(parts.join(""), W - 80), cx, 102, { align: "center", lineHeightFactor: 1.35 });

  const centers = [74, cx, 223];
  c.signers.forEach((signer, index) => {
    const center = centers[index] ?? cx;
    const image = images[index];
    if (image) {
      const props = doc.getImageProperties(image);
      const ratio = props.width / Math.max(props.height, 1);
      let width = 46;
      let height = width / ratio;
      if (height > 16) {
        height = 16;
        width = height * ratio;
      }
      doc.addImage(image, "PNG", center - width / 2, 156 - height, width, height);
    }
    doc.setDrawColor(...INK);
    doc.setLineWidth(0.3);
    doc.line(center - 28, 158, center + 28, 158);
    doc.setFont("times", "bold");
    doc.setTextColor(...INK);
    const signerName = signer.name.trim() || "Nome";
    fit(signerName, 56, 11, 8);
    doc.text(signerName, center, 164, { align: "center" });
    doc.setFont("helvetica", "normal");
    doc.setFontSize(8);
    doc.setTextColor(...MUTED);
    doc.text(signer.role, center, 169, { align: "center" });
  });

  doc.setFont("helvetica", "normal");
  doc.setFontSize(8);
  doc.setTextColor(...MUTED);
  doc.text(`Emitido em ${format(new Date(), "d 'de' MMMM 'de' yyyy", { locale: ptBR })} Â· RA ${c.ra}`, cx, 186, { align: "center" });

  doc.save(`Certificado-${slugify(c.event_title)}-${slugify(c.name)}.pdf`);
}


==================== src\lib\data.ts ====================
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { format, isSameDay, isSameMonth } from "date-fns";
import { ptBR } from "date-fns/locale";
import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
import type { Tables } from "@/integrations/supabase/types";

export type EventRow = Tables<"events">;
export type ScheduleRow = Tables<"event_schedule">;
export type ProductRow = Tables<"products">;
export type AnnouncementRow = Tables<"announcements">;
export type RegistrationRow = Tables<"registrations">;
export type OrderRow = Tables<"orders">;
export type BoardMemberRow = Tables<"board_members">;

export function usePublishedEvents() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ["events", "published"],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.from("events").select("*").eq("published", true).order("starts_at");
      if (error) throw error;
      return data;
    },
  });
}

export function useEvent(slug: string) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ["events", "slug", slug],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.from("events").select("*, event_schedule(*)").eq("slug", slug).maybeSingle();
      if (error) throw error;
      if (!data) return null;
      const { event_schedule, ...event } = data;
      return { event, schedule: [...event_schedule].sort((a, b) => a.sort_order - b.sort_order) };
    },
  });
}

export function useEventPhotos(eventId: string | undefined) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ["events", "photos", eventId],
    enabled: !!eventId,
    queryFn: async () => {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.from("event_photos").select("id, event_id, image_url, created_at").eq("event_id", eventId!).order("created_at");
      if (error) throw error;
      return data;
    },
  });
}

export function useSpotsTaken(eventId: string | undefined) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ["events", "spots", eventId],
    enabled: !!eventId,
    queryFn: async () => {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("event_spots_taken", { _event_id: eventId! });
      if (error) throw error;
      return data;
    },
  });
}

export function useActiveProducts() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ["products", "active"],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.from("products").select("*").eq("active", true).order("sort_order").order("created_at");
      if (error) throw error;
      return data;
    },
  });
}

export function useAnnouncements() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ["announcements", "published"],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.from("announcements").select("*").eq("published", true).order("sort_order").order("created_at", { ascending: false });
      if (error) throw error;
      return data;
    },
  });
}

export function useBoardMembers() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ["board-members", "active"],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.from("board_members").select("id, name, role, photo_url, sector, instagram, sort_order, active, in_chapa").eq("active", true).order("sort_order").order("created_at");
      if (error) throw error;
      return data;
    },
  });
}

/** Featured upcoming event first, otherwise the next upcoming one. */
export function pickHighlight(events: EventRow[]) {
  const now = Date.now();
  const upcoming = events.filter((e) => new Date(e.ends_at ?? e.starts_at).getTime() >= now);
  return upcoming.find((e) => e.featured) ?? upcoming[0] ?? null;
}

export const hasEnded = (event: Pick<EventRow, "starts_at" | "ends_at">) => new Date(event.ends_at ?? event.starts_at).getTime() < Date.now();

export function isRegistrationOpen(event: EventRow) {
  const deadlinePassed = event.registration_closes_at != null && new Date(event.registration_closes_at).getTime() <= Date.now();
  return event.registration_open && !deadlinePassed && !hasEnded(event);
}

export type EventCategory = EventRow["category"];
export const eventCategories: { id: EventCategory; label: string }[] = [
  { id: "faculdade", label: "Palestras e faculdade" },
  { id: "festa", label: "Festas" },
  { id: "esporte", label: "Esportes" },
  { id: "outro", label: "Outros" },
];
export const categoryLabel = (c: EventCategory) => eventCategories.find((x) => x.id === c)?.label ?? c;

export type ProductStatus = ProductRow["status"];
export const productStatusLabel: Record<ProductStatus, string> = { disponivel: "DisponÃ­vel", esgotado: "Esgotado", em_breve: "Em breve" };

/** Ended events whose certificate is released. */
export function useCertificateEvents() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ["events", "certificates"],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.from("events").select("id, slug, title, starts_at, ends_at, certificate_complementary_hours, certificate_mandatory_hours")
        .eq("published", true).eq("certificate_enabled", true).lte("starts_at", new Date().toISOString()).order("starts_at", { ascending: false });
      if (error) throw error;
      return data.filter(hasEnded);
    },
  });
}

/** Groups members by sector, keeping sectors in the order their first member appears. */
export function groupBySector<T extends { sector: string }>(members: T[]) {
  const groups = new Map<string, T[]>();
  for (const m of members) groups.set(m.sector, [...(groups.get(m.sector) ?? []), m]);
  return [...groups.entries()].map(([sector, items]) => ({ sector, items }));
}

export function formatEventDays(event: Pick<EventRow, "starts_at" | "ends_at">) {
  const start = new Date(event.starts_at);
  const end = event.ends_at ? new Date(event.ends_at) : null;
  if (!end || isSameDay(start, end)) return format(start, "d 'de' MMMM", { locale: ptBR });
  if (isSameMonth(start, end)) return `${format(start, "d")}â€“${format(end, "d 'de' MMMM", { locale: ptBR })}`;
  return `${format(start, "d 'de' MMM", { locale: ptBR })} â€“ ${format(end, "d 'de' MMM", { locale: ptBR })}`;
}

export function formatEventHours(event: Pick<EventRow, "starts_at" | "ends_at">) {
  const hour = (d: Date) => (d.getMinutes() ? format(d, "H'h'mm") : format(d, "H'h'"));
  const start = new Date(event.starts_at);
  return event.ends_at ? `${hour(start)} Ã s ${hour(new Date(event.ends_at))}` : hour(start);
}

export const formatDateTime = (value: string) => format(new Date(value), "dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm");

export const slugify = (value: string) =>
  value.normalize("NFD").replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "").toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-").replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "");

export type MediaFolder = "events" | "products" | "board" | "pages" | "gallery" | "signatures";

export async function uploadMedia(file: File, folder: MediaFolder) {
  const ext = file.name.split(".").pop()?.toLowerCase() ?? "jpg";
  const path = `${folder}/${crypto.randomUUID()}.${ext}`;
  const { error } = await supabase.storage.from("media").upload(path, file, { contentType: file.type });
  if (error) throw error;
  return supabase.storage.from("media").getPublicUrl(path).data.publicUrl;
}


==================== src\lib\error-capture.ts ====================
// Captures the original Error out-of-band so server.ts can recover the stack
// when h3 has already swallowed the throw into a generic 500 Response.

let lastCapturedError: { error: unknown; at: number } | undefined;
const TTL_MS = 5_000;

function record(error: unknown) {
  lastCapturedError = { error, at: Date.now() };
}

// h3's HTTPError serializes to {"status":500,"unhandled":true,"message":"HTTPError"} â€”
// no stack, no cause â€” so a plain console.error(error) reaches the log pipeline with
// the failure detail stripped. Expand Error-like args into a string that keeps the
// message, stack, and the full cause chain.
const CAUSE_DEPTH_LIMIT = 5;
const DESCRIPTION_LENGTH_LIMIT = 8_000;

export function describeError(error: unknown): string {
  const parts: string[] = [];
  let current: unknown = error;
  for (let depth = 0; depth < CAUSE_DEPTH_LIMIT && current != null; depth++) {
    if (!(current instanceof Error)) {
      parts.push(typeof current === "string" ? current : safeStringify(current));
      break;
    }
    const label = depth === 0 ? "" : "caused by: ";
    const status = describeStatus(current);
    parts.push(`${label}${current.stack ?? `${current.name}: ${current.message}`}${status}`);
    current = current.cause;
  }
  return parts.join("\n").slice(0, DESCRIPTION_LENGTH_LIMIT);
}

function describeStatus(error: Error): string {
  const { status, statusCode } = error as { status?: unknown; statusCode?: unknown };
  const value = status ?? statusCode;
  return typeof value === "number" ? ` (status ${value})` : "";
}

function safeStringify(value: unknown): string {
  try {
    return JSON.stringify(value) ?? String(value);
  } catch {
    return String(value);
  }
}

function isErrorLike(value: unknown): value is Error {
  return value instanceof Error;
}

// Wrap console.error so errors logged by any layer â€” including h3's internal
// unhandled-error logging, which this file cannot hook directly â€” are both
// recorded for consumeLastCapturedError and expanded before serialization.
const originalConsoleError = console.error.bind(console);
console.error = (...args: unknown[]) => {
  const expanded = args.map((arg) => {
    if (!isErrorLike(arg)) return arg;
    record(arg);
    return describeError(arg);
  });
  originalConsoleError(...expanded);
};

if (typeof globalThis.addEventListener === "function") {
  globalThis.addEventListener("error", (event) => record((event as ErrorEvent).error ?? event));
  globalThis.addEventListener("unhandledrejection", (event) =>
    record((event as PromiseRejectionEvent).reason),
  );
}

export function consumeLastCapturedError(): unknown {
  if (!lastCapturedError) return undefined;
  if (Date.now() - lastCapturedError.at > TTL_MS) {
    lastCapturedError = undefined;
    return undefined;
  }
  const { error } = lastCapturedError;
  lastCapturedError = undefined;
  return error;
}

==================== src\lib\masks.ts ====================
const digits = (value: string, max: number) => value.replace(/\D/g, "").slice(0, max);

export function maskCpf(value: string) {
  const d = digits(value, 11);
  return d.replace(/^(\d{3})(\d)/, "$1.$2").replace(/^(\d{3})\.(\d{3})(\d)/, "$1.$2.$3").replace(/\.(\d{3})(\d{1,2})$/, ".$1-$2");
}

export function maskPhone(value: string) {
  const d = digits(value, 11);
  if (d.length <= 2) return d.length ? `(${d}` : "";
  if (d.length <= 6) return `(${d.slice(0, 2)}) ${d.slice(2)}`;
  if (d.length <= 10) return `(${d.slice(0, 2)}) ${d.slice(2, 6)}-${d.slice(6)}`;
  return `(${d.slice(0, 2)}) ${d.slice(2, 7)}-${d.slice(7)}`;
}

export function pageText(content: Record<PageKey, string> | undefined, key: PageKey) {
  if (key === "fallback_image" || key === "site_logo") return content?.[key]?.trim() ?? "";
  return content?.[key]?.trim() || pageDefaults[key];
}

export async function fetchPageContent() {
  const { data, error } = await supabase.from("page_content").select("key, value");
  if (error) throw error;
  const content = { ...pageDefaults };
  for (const row of data) {
    if (row.key in content) content[row.key as PageKey] = row.value;
  }
  return content;
}

export type PageContentPayload = { content: Record<PageKey, string>; fetchedAt: number };

function isPageContentPayload(value: unknown): value is PageContentPayload {
  if (!value || typeof value !== "object" || !("content" in value) || !("fetchedAt" in value)) return false;
  const content = (value as { content: unknown }).content;
  return !!content && typeof content === "object" && "site_logo" in content && "site_name" in content;
}

export function usePageContent() {
  const payload = useRouterState({
    select: (state) => state.matches.map((match) => match.loaderData).find(isPageContentPayload),
  });
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ["page-content"],
    queryFn: fetchPageContent,
    staleTime: 30_000,
    ...(payload ? { initialData: payload.content, initialDataUpdatedAt: payload.fetchedAt } : {}),
  });
}

const paidStatuses: OrderStatus[] = ["pago", "em_producao", "aguardando_retirada", "entregue"];

function Overview() {
  const { data } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["admin", "overview"],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const [events, registrations, products, orders] = await Promise.all([
        supabase.from("events").select("id, title, starts_at, ends_at, capacity, published, registrations(count)").order("starts_at"),
        supabase.from("registrations").select("id", { count: "exact", head: true }),
        supabase.from("products").select("id", { count: "exact", head: true }).eq("active", true),
        supabase.from("orders").select("status, total, order_items(product_name, size, quantity)"),
      ]);
      return { events: events.data ?? [], registrations: registrations.count ?? 0, products: products.count ?? 0, orders: orders.data ?? [] };
    },
  });
  const now = Date.now();
  const upcoming = (data?.events ?? []).filter((e) => new Date(e.ends_at ?? e.starts_at).getTime() >= now);
  const paid = (data?.orders ?? []).filter((o) => paidStatuses.includes(o.status));
  const revenue = paid.reduce((sum, o) => sum + Number(o.total), 0);
  const production = new Map<string, Map<string, number>>();
  for (const o of paid) for (const it of o.order_items) {
    const sizes = production.get(it.product_name) ?? new Map<string, number>();
    sizes.set(it.size ?? "Ãšnico", (sizes.get(it.size ?? "Ãšnico") ?? 0) + it.quantity);
    production.set(it.product_name, sizes);
  }

  return <>
    <div className="mt-6 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 lg:grid-cols-4">
      <Metric icon={CalendarDays} label="PrÃ³ximos eventos" value={String(upcoming.length)} hint={`${data?.events.filter((e) => e.published).length ?? 0} publicados no total`} />
      <Metric icon={UsersRound} label="Inscritos" value={String(data?.registrations ?? 0)} hint="em todos os eventos" />
      <Metric icon={Package} label="Produtos na loja" value={String(data?.products ?? 0)} hint="visÃ­veis no catÃ¡logo" />
      <Metric icon={CircleDollarSign} label="Total arrecadado" value={money(revenue)} hint={`${paid.length} pedido${paid.length === 1 ? "" : "s"} pago${paid.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}`} />
    </div>
    <div className="mt-7 grid gap-7 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_340px]">
      <section className="min-w-0 rounded-xl border border-border bg-card">
        <div className="border-b border-border p-5"><h2 className="text-lg font-bold">InscriÃ§Ãµes por evento</h2><p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">PrÃ³ximos eventos</p></div>
        <div className="space-y-5 p-5">
          {upcoming.map((e) => { const count = e.registrations[0]?.count ?? 0; const pct = e.capacity ? Math.min(100, Math.round((count / e.capacity) * 100)) : null; return <div key={e.id}>
            <div className="flex justify-between gap-3 text-sm font-bold"><span className="truncate">{e.title}{!e.published && <span className="ml-2 text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground">(rascunho)</span>}</span><span className="shrink-0">{count}{e.capacity ? ` / ${e.capacity}` : ""}</span></div>
            <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{formatEventDays(e)}</p>
            {pct !== null && <div className="mt-2 h-2 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-muted"><div className="h-full rounded-full bg-gold" style={{ width: `${pct}%` }} /></div>}
          </div>; })}
          {upcoming.length === 0 && <p className="text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">Nenhum evento programado.</p>}
        </div>
      </section>
      <aside className="rounded-xl border border-border bg-card p-5">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3"><span className="grid size-10 place-items-center rounded-lg bg-navy-soft text-primary"><PackageCheck size={20} /></span><div><h2 className="font-bold">Resumo de produÃ§Ã£o</h2><p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Pedidos pagos</p></div></div>
        <div className="mt-6 space-y-5">
          {[...production].map(([product, sizes]) => <div key={product}><div className="flex justify-between text-sm font-bold"><span>{product}</span><span>{[...sizes.values()].reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0)} un.</span></div><p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{[...sizes].map(([s, n]) => `${n} ${s}`).join(" Â· ")}</p></div>)}
          {production.size === 0 && <p className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground"><ShoppingBag size={16} /> Sem pedidos pagos ainda.</p>}
        </div>
      </aside>
    </div>
  </>;
}

function Metric({ icon: Icon, label, value, hint }: { icon: typeof CircleDollarSign; label: string; value: string; hint: string }) {
  return <article className="rounded-xl border border-border bg-card p-4 sm:p-5"><Icon size={21} className="text-gold" /><p className="mt-4 text-xs font-bold uppercase text-muted-foreground">{label}</p><p className="mt-1 truncate text-2xl font-extrabold">{value}</p><p className="mt-1 text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground">{hint}</p></article>;
}


==================== src\routes\_authenticated\route.tsx ====================
import { createFileRoute, Outlet, redirect } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";

export const Route = createFileRoute("/_authenticated")({
  ssr: false,
  beforeLoad: async ({ location }) => {
    const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
    if (error || !data.user) throw redirect({ to: "/auth", search: { redirect: location.pathname } });
    return { user: data.user };
  },
  component: () => <Outlet />,
});


==================== src\routes\auth.tsx ====================
import { createFileRoute, useNavigate } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { BrandMark } from "@/components/app-shell";
import { z } from "zod";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
import { safeRedirect } from "@/lib/auth";

export const Route = createFileRoute("/auth")({
  validateSearch: z.object({ redirect: z.string().optional() }),
  head: () => ({ meta: [
    { title: "Entrar â€” Sistema Themis" }, { name: "description", content: "Acesso restrito Ã  diretoria do DA." },
    { property: "og:title", content: "Entrar â€” Sistema Themis" }, { property: "og:description", content: "Login da diretoria do DA de Direito." },
    { property: "og:type", content: "website" }, { name: "twitter:card", content: "summary_large_image" }, { name: "robots", content: "noindex" },
  ] }),
  component: AuthPage,
});

const input = "h-12 rounded-lg border border-input bg-background px-3 font-normal outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-ring";

function AuthPage() {
  const { redirect } = Route.useSearch();
  const target = safeRedirect(redirect);
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<"in" | "forgot">("in");
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState<{ ok: boolean; text: string } | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    supabase.auth.getSession().then(({ data }) => { if (data.session) navigate({ to: target, replace: true }); });
    const { data: sub } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((event, session) => {
      if (event === "SIGNED_IN" && session) navigate({ to: target, replace: true });
    });
    return () => sub.subscription.unsubscribe();
  }, [navigate, target]);

  const submit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault(); setBusy(true); setMsg(null);
    try {
      if (mode === "in") {
        const { error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({ email, password });
        if (error) setMsg({ ok: false, text: "E-mail ou senha incorretos." });
      } else {
        const { error } = await supabase.auth.resetPasswordForEmail(email, { redirectTo: `${window.location.origin}/reset-password` });
        setMsg(error ? { ok: false, text: "NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel enviar o link agora. Tente novamente em alguns minutos." } : { ok: true, text: "Se o e-mail tiver acesso ao painel, vocÃª vai receber um link para redefinir a senha." });
      }
    } finally { setBusy(false); }
  };

  return <div className="app-container grid min-h-[70vh] place-items-center py-10">
    <div className="w-full max-w-md rounded-xl border border-border bg-card p-6 shadow-sm">
      <BrandMark className="mx-auto size-12" icon={22} />
      <h1 className="mt-4 text-center text-2xl font-bold">{mode === "in" ? "Acesso da diretoria" : "Recuperar senha"}</h1>
      <p className="mt-2 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">Ãrea restrita aos membros da diretoria do DA. As contas sÃ£o criadas pelo responsÃ¡vel pela plataforma.</p>
      <form onSubmit={submit} className="mt-6">
        <label className="grid gap-1.5 text-sm font-bold">E-mail<input required type="email" autoComplete="email" value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} className={input} /></label>
        {mode === "in" && <label className="mt-4 grid gap-1.5 text-sm font-bold">Senha<input required type="password" autoComplete="current-password" value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} className={input} /></label>}
        {msg && <p className={`mt-3 rounded-lg p-3 text-xs font-bold ${msg.ok ? "bg-success-soft text-success" : "bg-destructive/10 text-destructive"}`}>{msg.text}</p>}
        <Button size="lg" className="mt-5 w-full" type="submit" disabled={busy}>{mode === "in" ? "Entrar" : "Enviar link"}</Button>
      </form>
      <div className="mt-4 text-center text-sm">
        {mode === "in"
          ? <button className="text-muted-foreground" onClick={() => { setMode("forgot"); setMsg(null); }}>Esqueci a senha</button>
          : <button className="font-bold text-primary" onClick={() => { setMode("in"); setMsg(null); }}>Voltar para o login</button>}
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>;
}


==================== src\routes\certificados.tsx ====================
import { createFileRoute } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { Award, Download } from "lucide-react";
import { useState } from "react";
import { z } from "zod";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
import { type CertificateData, downloadCertificate, readSigners } from "@/lib/certificate";
import { formatEventDays, useCertificateEvents } from "@/lib/data";
import { pageText, usePageContent } from "@/lib/pages";

const searchSchema = z.object({ evento: z.string().optional().catch(undefined) });

export const Route = createFileRoute("/certificados")({
  validateSearch: searchSchema,
  head: () => ({ meta: [
    { title: "Certificados â€” Sistema Themis" }, { name: "description", content: "Emita o certificado dos eventos do DA de Direito em que vocÃª esteve presente." },
    { property: "og:title", content: "Certificados â€” Sistema Themis" }, { property: "og:description", content: "Baixe seu certificado de participaÃ§Ã£o." }, { property: "og:type", content: "website" }, { name: "twitter:card", content: "summary_large_image" },
  ] }),
  component: CertificatesPage,
});

type HourType = "complementar" | "obrigatoria";
type Result = ({ status: "ok" } & CertificateData) | { status: "unavailable" | "not_found" | "no_attendance" | "year_restricted" | "hours_missing" | "signatures_missing" | "invalid" };

const errorText: Record<Exclude<Result["status"], "ok">, string> = {
  unavailable: "O certificado deste evento ainda nÃ£o foi liberado.",
  not_found: "NÃ£o encontramos uma inscriÃ§Ã£o com esse e-mail e RA neste evento. Confira os dados usados na inscriÃ§Ã£o.",
  no_attendance: "Encontramos sua inscriÃ§Ã£o, mas a presenÃ§a nÃ£o foi confirmada neste evento. Fale com a diretoria do DA se isso estiver errado.",
  year_restricted: "Do 1Âº ao 3Âº ano o certificado sai apenas com horas complementares.",
  hours_missing: "A diretoria ainda nÃ£o definiu essa quantidade de horas para o evento.",
  signatures_missing: "A diretoria ainda nÃ£o concluiu as assinaturas deste certificado.",
  invalid: "Confira o ano e o tipo de horas.",
};

const years = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5] as const;

const input = "h-12 w-full rounded-lg border border-input bg-background px-3 font-normal outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-ring";

function CertificatesPage() {
  const { evento } = Route.useSearch();
  const { data: events = [], isLoading } = useCertificateEvents();
  const { data: pages } = usePageContent();
  const [picked, setPicked] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [ra, setRa] = useState("");
  const [year, setYear] = useState<(typeof years)[number]>(1);
  const [hourType, setHourType] = useState<HourType>("complementar");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState<{ text: string; ok: boolean } | null>(null);
  const eventId = picked ?? events.find((e) => e.slug === evento)?.id ?? events[0]?.id ?? "";
  const selected = events.find((ev) => ev.id === eventId);
  const canChoose = year >= 4 && selected?.certificate_mandatory_hours != null;
  const chosenType: HourType = canChoose ? hourType : "complementar";

  const submit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault(); setBusy(true); setMessage(null);
    const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("issue_certificate", { _event_id: eventId, _email: email, _ra: ra, _year: year, _hour_type: chosenType });
    const result = data as Result | null;
    if (error || !result) { setBusy(false); return setMessage({ text: "NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel gerar agora. Tente novamente.", ok: false }); }
    if (result.status !== "ok") { setBusy(false); return setMessage({ text: errorText[result.status], ok: false }); }
    const signers = readSigners(result.signers);
    if (!signers) { setBusy(false); return setMessage({ text: "NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel montar o PDF. Tente novamente.", ok: false }); }
    try {
      await downloadCertificate({ ...result, hours: Number(result.hours), hour_type: result.hour_type === "obrigatoria" ? "obrigatoria" : "complementar", signers });
      setMessage({ text: "Pronto! O download do seu certificado comeÃ§ou.", ok: true });
    } catch {
      setMessage({ text: "NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel montar o PDF. Tente novamente.", ok: false });
    }
    setBusy(false);
  };

  return <div className="app-container grid min-h-[70vh] place-items-center py-10">
    <div className="w-full max-w-md rounded-xl border border-border bg-card p-6 shadow-sm">
      <span className="grid size-12 place-items-center rounded-lg bg-primary text-gold"><Award size={22} /></span>
      <h1 className="mt-4 text-2xl font-bold">{pageText(pages, "certificates_title")}</h1>
      <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{pageText(pages, "certificates_text")}</p>
      {isLoading ? <div className="mt-6 h-40 animate-pulse rounded-lg bg-muted" /> : events.length === 0
        ? <p className="mt-6 rounded-lg bg-muted p-4 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">{pageText(pages, "certificates_empty")}</p>
        : <form onSubmit={submit} className="mt-6 grid gap-3">
          <label className="grid gap-1.5 text-sm font-bold">Evento
            <select required value={eventId} onChange={(e) => setPicked(e.target.value)} className={input}>
              {events.map((ev) => <option key={ev.id} value={ev.id}>{ev.title} Â· {formatEventDays(ev)}</option>)}
            </select>
          </label>
          <label className="grid gap-1.5 text-sm font-bold">E-mail da inscriÃ§Ã£o<input required type="email" value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} placeholder="voce@exemplo.com" autoComplete="email" className={input} /></label>
          <label className="grid gap-1.5 text-sm font-bold">NÃºmero do RA<input required maxLength={30} value={ra} onChange={(e) => setRa(e.target.value)} placeholder="202600000" className={input} /></label>
          <label className="grid gap-1.5 text-sm font-bold">Ano do curso
            <select required value={year} onChange={(e) => setYear(Number(e.target.value) as (typeof years)[number])} className={input}>
              {years.map((item) => <option key={item} value={item}>{item}Âº ano</option>)}
            </select>
          </label>
          {year <= 3 && <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Do 1Âº ao 3Âº ano o certificado sai com horas complementares{selected?.certificate_complementary_hours != null ? ` (${String(selected.certificate_complementary_hours).replace(".", ",")}h)` : ""}.</p>}
          {year >= 4 && canChoose && <fieldset className="grid gap-2"><legend className="text-sm font-bold">Tipo de horas</legend>
            <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm"><input type="radio" name="hour-type" checked={hourType === "complementar"} onChange={() => setHourType("complementar")} className="size-4 accent-gold" />Complementares Â· {String(selected?.certificate_complementary_hours ?? "").replace(".", ",")}h</label>
            <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm"><input type="radio" name="hour-type" checked={hourType === "obrigatoria"} onChange={() => setHourType("obrigatoria")} className="size-4 accent-gold" />ObrigatÃ³rias Â· {String(selected?.certificate_mandatory_hours ?? "").replace(".", ",")}h</label>
          </fieldset>}
          {year >= 4 && !canChoose && <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">As horas obrigatÃ³rias ainda nÃ£o foram definidas para este evento. O certificado sai com as horas complementares.</p>}
          {message && <p className={`rounded-lg p-3 text-xs font-bold ${message.ok ? "bg-success-soft text-success" : "bg-destructive/10 text-destructive"}`}>{message.text}</p>}
          <Button size="lg" type="submit" disabled={busy || !eventId} className="mt-1 w-full"><Download /> {busy ? "Gerando..." : "Baixar certificado"}</Button>
        </form>}
    </div>
  </div>;
}


==================== src\routes\checkout.tsx ====================
import { createFileRoute, Link } from "@tanstack/react-router";

const searchSchema = z.object({ item: z.string().catch("") });
export const Route = createFileRoute("/checkout")({ validateSearch: (search) => searchSchema.parse(search), head: () => ({ meta: [
  { title: "Finalizar pedido â€” Sistema Themis" }, { name: "description", content: "Finalize seu pedido." }, { property: "og:title", content: "Finalizar pedido â€” Sistema Themis" }, { property: "og:description", content: "Pagamento simples e seguro." }, { property: "og:type", content: "website" }, { name: "twitter:card", content: "summary_large_image" },
] }), component: CheckoutPage });

const CARD_FEE = 0.0349;
const PIX_CODE = "00020126580014BR.GOV.BCB.PIX0136themis-da-direito-20265204000053039865802BR5913DA DIREITO6009RIBEIRAO62070503***6304A1F2";

function CheckoutPage() {
  const { item } = Route.useSearch();
  const { data: products, isLoading } = useActiveProducts();
  const [method, setMethod] = useState<"pix" | "card">("pix");
  const [stage, setStage] = useState<"form" | "paid" | "declined">("form");
  const [consent, setConsent] = useState(false);
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
  const [installments, setInstallments] = useState(1);
  const [size, setSize] = useState("");
  const product = products?.find((x) => x.id === item && x.status === "disponivel");

  if (isLoading) return <div className="app-container py-12"><div className="h-96 animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-muted" /></div>;
  if (!product) return <div className="app-container grid min-h-[60vh] place-items-center py-12 text-center"><div><h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Produto indisponÃ­vel</h1><p className="mt-2 text-muted-foreground">Escolha um produto na loja para continuar.</p><Button asChild className="mt-6"><Link to="/produtos">Ir para a loja</Link></Button></div></div>;

  const base = Number(product.price);
  const fee = method === "card" ? base * CARD_FEE : 0;
  const total = base + fee;
  const chosenSize = size || (product.sizes.length === 1 ? product.sizes[0] : "");

  if (stage === "paid") return <div className="app-container grid min-h-[70vh] place-items-center py-12"><div className="max-w-lg text-center"><span className="mx-auto grid size-20 place-items-center rounded-full bg-success-soft text-success"><CheckCircle2 size={42} /></span><h1 className="mt-5 text-3xl font-bold">Pagamento aprovado!</h1><p className="mt-3 text-muted-foreground">Esta Ã© uma demonstraÃ§Ã£o: nenhum pedido foi registrado e nenhuma cobranÃ§a foi feita.</p><p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">Quando as vendas online forem ativadas, vocÃª recebe a confirmaÃ§Ã£o e o aviso de retirada na sede do DA.</p><Button asChild size="lg" className="mt-7 w-full"><Link to="/produtos">Voltar para a loja</Link></Button></div></div>;

  if (stage === "declined") return <div className="app-container grid min-h-[70vh] place-items-center py-12"><div className="max-w-lg text-center"><span className="mx-auto grid size-20 place-items-center rounded-full bg-destructive/10 text-destructive"><TriangleAlert size={42} /></span><h1 className="mt-5 text-3xl font-bold">Pagamento recusado</h1><p className="mt-3 text-muted-foreground">A operadora nÃ£o autorizou a transaÃ§Ã£o. Verifique os dados do cartÃ£o ou escolha outra forma de pagamento. Nenhuma cobranÃ§a foi realizada.</p><div className="mt-7 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2"><Button size="lg" variant="outline" onClick={() => setStage("form")}>Tentar novamente</Button><Button size="lg" onClick={() => { setMethod("pix"); setStage("form"); }}>Pagar com Pix</Button></div></div></div>;

"border-primary bg-navy-soft text-primary" : "border-border bg-background"}`}>{s}</button>)}</div>
        </section>}

                <Button type="button" variant="outline" size="icon" className="size-11 shrink-0" aria-label="Copiar cÃ³digo Pix" onClick={() => { navigator.clipboard?.writeText(PIX_CODE).catch(() => undefined); setCopied(true); window.setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000); }}>{copied ? <CheckCircle2 size={18} className="text-success" /> : <Copy size={18} />}</Button>
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="mt-5 grid gap-4">

                  {[1, 2, 3].map((n) => <option key={n} value={n}>{n}x de {money(total / n)}</option>)}
                </select>
              </label>
              <p className="rounded-lg bg-muted p-3 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Juros e taxas da operaÃ§Ã£o (3,49%) sÃ£o acrescidos ao valor final pago pelo aluno. Nenhum dado do cartÃ£o fica armazenado neste site.</p>

          )}
        </section>
        <section className="space-y-3">
          <Notice icon={RefreshCcw} title="Sem trocas ou devoluÃ§Ãµes" text="Produtos confeccionados sob encomenda. Confira a grade antes de finalizar: nÃ£o hÃ¡ devoluÃ§Ã£o por erro na escolha do tamanho." />

        </section>
      </div>
      <aside className="h-fit rounded-xl border border-border bg-card p-5 shadow-sm lg:sticky lg:top-24">
        <h2 className="text-lg font-bold">Resumo</h2>
        <div className="mt-4 space-y-2 border-b border-border pb-4 text-sm">
          <div className="flex justify-between gap-4"><span className="font-semibold">{product.name}{chosenSize && ` Â· ${chosenSize}`}</span><span className="font-bold">{money(base)}</span></div>
          {method === "card" && <div className="flex justify-between gap-4 text-muted-foreground"><span>Taxas do cartÃ£o (3,49%)</span><span>{money(fee)}</span></div>}
