        <label className="flex items-start gap-2.5 rounded-lg border border-border bg-muted/60 p-3 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">
          <input type="checkbox" checked={consent} onChange={(e) => setConsent(e.target.checked)} className="mt-0.5 size-4 accent-gold" />
          <span>Li e aceito os <strong className="text-foreground">Termos de Uso</strong> e a <strong className="text-foreground">PolÃ­tica de Privacidade</strong>, e autorizo o uso do meu nome, CPF e e-mail para o processamento deste pedido (LGPD).</span>
        </label>
        <Button size="lg" className="mt-4 w-full" type="submit" disabled={!consent || (product.sizes.length > 1 && !chosenSize)}>Confirmar pagamento</Button>
        {method === "card" && <button type="button" onClick={() => setStage("declined")} className="mt-2 w-full text-center text-xs font-bold text-muted-foreground underline underline-offset-2">Simular pagamento recusado</button>}
        <p className="mt-3 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground"><ShieldCheck size={15} /> Ambiente de pagamento simulado</p>
      </aside>
    </form>
  </div>;
}

function Field({ label, placeholder, type = "text" }: { label: string; placeholder: string; type?: string }) {
  return <label className="grid gap-1.5 text-sm font-bold">{label}<input required type={type} className="h-12 rounded-lg border border-input bg-background px-3 font-normal outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-ring" placeholder={placeholder} /></label>;
}
function Method({ active, onClick, icon: Icon, title, subtitle }: { active: boolean; onClick: () => void; icon: typeof QrCode; title: string; subtitle: string }) {
  return <button type="button" onClick={onClick} className={`flex h-20 flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 rounded-lg border font-bold ${active ? "border-primary bg-navy-soft text-primary" : "border-border bg-background"}`}><Icon size={23} />{title}<span className="text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase text-muted-foreground">{subtitle}</span></button>;
}
function Notice({ icon: Icon, title, text }: { icon: typeof MapPin; title: string; text: string }) {
  return <div className="flex gap-3 rounded-xl border border-gold/40 bg-card p-4"><Icon size={20} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0 text-gold" /><div className="text-sm"><strong>{title}</strong><p className="mt-0.5 leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">{text}</p></div></div>;
}


==================== src\routes\eventos.$slug.tsx ====================
import { createFileRoute, Link } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { Award, CalendarDays, CheckCircle2, Clock3, Images, MapPin, ShieldCheck, UsersRound } from "lucide-react";
import { useState } from "react";
import { z } from "zod";
import { EventGallery } from "@/components/event-gallery";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
import { type EventRow, categoryLabel, formatDateTime, formatEventDays, formatEventHours, hasEnded, isRegistrationOpen, useEvent, useSpotsTaken } from "@/lib/data";
import { pageText, usePageContent } from "@/lib/pages";
import hero from "@/assets/semana-juridica.jpg";

const searchSchema = z.object({ aba: z.enum(["fotos"]).optional().catch(undefined) });

export const Route = createFileRoute("/eventos/$slug")({ validateSearch: searchSchema, head: () => ({ meta: [
  { title: "Evento â€” Sistema Themis" }, { name: "description", content: "InscriÃ§Ã£o gratuita em evento do DA de Direito." }, { property: "og:type", content: "website" }, { name: "twitter:card", content: "summary_large_image" },
] }), component: EventPage });

function EventPage() {
  const { slug } = Route.useParams();
  const { aba } = Route.useSearch();
  const navigate = Route.useNavigate();
  const { data, isLoading } = useEvent(slug);
  const { data: taken } = useSpotsTaken(data?.event.id);
  const { data: pages } = usePageContent();

  if (isLoading) return <div className="app-container py-10"><div className="aspect-[16/9] w-full animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-muted" /></div>;
  if (!data) return <div className="app-container grid min-h-[60vh] place-items-center py-10 text-center"><div><h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Evento nÃ£o encontrado</h1><p className="mt-2 text-muted-foreground">Ele pode ter sido removido ou ainda nÃ£o foi publicado.</p><Button asChild className="mt-6"><Link to="/eventos">Ver todos os eventos</Link></Button></div></div>;

  const { event, schedule } = data;
  const remaining = event.capacity != null && taken != null ? Math.max(event.capacity - taken, 0) : null;
  const tab = aba === "fotos" ? "fotos" : "sobre";
  const choose = (next: "sobre" | "fotos") => navigate({ search: next === "fotos" ? { aba: "fotos" } : {}, replace: true });
  return <div className="app-container py-5 sm:py-10">
    <img src={event.image_url || pageText(pages, "fallback_image") || hero} alt={event.title} width={1600} height={912} className="aspect-[16/9] w-full rounded-xl object-cover grayscale" />
    <span className="mt-6 block text-sm font-bold uppercase text-gold">{categoryLabel(event.category)} Â· Gratuito</span>
    <h1 className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold sm:text-4xl">{event.title}</h1>
    <div className="mt-5 grid gap-3 text-sm sm:grid-cols-3"><Info icon={CalendarDays} text={formatEventDays(event)} /><Info icon={Clock3} text={formatEventHours(event)} />{event.location && <Info icon={MapPin} text={event.location} />}</div>
    <div className="mt-6 flex gap-2" role="tablist" aria-label="ConteÃºdo do evento">
      {(["sobre", "fotos"] as const).map((id) => <button key={id} type="button" role="tab" aria-selected={tab === id} onClick={() => choose(id)} className={`inline-flex h-10 items-center gap-2 rounded-full border px-4 text-sm font-bold ${tab === id ? "border-primary bg-primary text-primary-foreground" : "border-border bg-card text-muted-foreground"}`}>{id === "fotos" && <Images size={16} />}{id === "sobre" ? "Sobre" : "Fotos"}</button>)}
    </div>
    {tab === "fotos" ? <EventGallery eventId={event.id} title={event.title} /> : <div className="mt-7 grid gap-8 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_400px]">
      <div>
        {(event.description || event.summary) && <><h2 className="text-xl font-bold">Sobre o evento</h2><p className="mt-3 whitespace-pre-line leading-7 text-muted-foreground">{event.description || event.summary}</p></>}
        {schedule.length > 0 && <><h2 className="mt-7 text-xl font-bold">ProgramaÃ§Ã£o</h2><div className="mt-3 space-y-3">{schedule.map((s) => <div key={s.id} className="rounded-lg border border-border bg-card p-4 font-semibold">{s.label}</div>)}</div></>}
      </div>
      <aside className="h-fit rounded-xl border border-border bg-card p-5 shadow-sm lg:sticky lg:top-24">
        <RegistrationPanel event={event} remaining={remaining} />
      </aside>
    </div>}
  </div>;
}

type Result = { status: "ok" | "duplicate" | "full" | "closed" | "not_found" | "invalid" };

const errorText: Record<Exclude<Result["status"], "ok">, string> = {
  duplicate: "JÃ¡ existe uma inscriÃ§Ã£o neste evento com esse e-mail ou RA.",
  full: "As vagas deste evento acabaram.",
  closed: "As inscriÃ§Ãµes para este evento estÃ£o encerradas.",
  not_found: "Evento nÃ£o encontrado.",
  invalid: "Confira os dados: nome completo, e-mail vÃ¡lido e RA.",
};

const input = "h-12 rounded-lg border border-input bg-background px-3 font-normal outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-ring";

function RegistrationPanel({ event, remaining }: { event: EventRow; remaining: number | null }) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const [form, setForm] = useState({ name: "", email: "", ra: "", website: "" });
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [done, setDone] = useState(false);
  const [emailSent, setEmailSent] = useState(false);
  const set = (key: keyof typeof form) => (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, [key]: e.target.value }));

  if (done) return <div className="text-center">
    <span className="mx-auto grid size-16 place-items-center rounded-full bg-success-soft text-success"><CheckCircle2 size={34} /></span>
    <h2 className="mt-4 text-xl font-bold">InscriÃ§Ã£o confirmada!</h2>
    <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground"><strong className="text-foreground">{form.name}</strong>, sua inscriÃ§Ã£o foi registrada. A presenÃ§a serÃ¡ marcada pela organizaÃ§Ã£o no dia do evento.</p>
    {emailSent && <p className="mt-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground">Enviamos a confirmaÃ§Ã£o para <strong className="text-foreground">{form.email}</strong> e mandaremos um lembrete na vÃ©spera.</p>}
  </div>;

  if (hasEnded(event)) return <div className="text-center">
    <h2 className="text-lg font-bold">Evento encerrado</h2>
    {event.certificate_enabled
      ? <><p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">Esteve presente? Baixe seu certificado com o e-mail e o RA da inscriÃ§Ã£o.</p><Button asChild className="mt-4 w-full"><Link to="/certificados" search={{ evento: event.slug }}><Award /> Emitir certificado</Link></Button></>
      : <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">Obrigado a todos que participaram!</p>}
  </div>;
  if (!isRegistrationOpen(event)) return <div className="text-center"><h2 className="text-lg font-bold">InscriÃ§Ãµes encerradas</h2><p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">Este evento nÃ£o estÃ¡ recebendo novas inscriÃ§Ãµes.</p></div>;
  if (remaining === 0) return <div className="text-center"><h2 className="text-lg font-bold">Vagas esgotadas</h2><p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">Todas as vagas deste evento jÃ¡ foram preenchidas.</p></div>;

  const submit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault(); setBusy(true); setError("");
    const { data, error: rpcError } = await supabase.rpc("register_for_event", {
      _event_id: event.id, _full_name: form.name, _email: form.email, _ra: form.ra, _website: form.website,
    });
    setBusy(false);
    const result = data as Result | null;
    if (rpcError || !result) return setError("NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel concluir a inscriÃ§Ã£o. Tente novamente.");
    if (result.status === "ok") {
      setDone(true);
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["events", "spots", event.id] });
      supabase.functions.invoke("event-emails", { body: { type: "confirmation", event_id: event.id, email: form.email } })
        .then(({ data: res }) => setEmailSent(Boolean(res?.sent)))
        .catch(() => undefined);
      return;
    }
    setError(errorText[result.status]);
  };

  return <form onSubmit={submit}>
    <h2 className="text-lg font-bold">FaÃ§a sua inscriÃ§Ã£o</h2>
    <p className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground"><UsersRound size={14} />{remaining != null ? `${remaining} vaga${remaining === 1 ? "" : "s"} restante${remaining === 1 ? "" : "s"}` : "InscriÃ§Ã£o gratuita"}</p>
    {event.registration_closes_at && <p className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground"><Clock3 size={14} />InscriÃ§Ãµes atÃ© {formatDateTime(event.registration_closes_at).replace(" ", " Ã s ")}</p>}
    <div className="mt-4 grid gap-3">
      <div aria-hidden="true" className="absolute -left-[9999px] h-px w-px overflow-hidden">
        <label>Deixe este campo em branco<input tabIndex={-1} autoComplete="off" value={form.website} onChange={set("website")} name="website" /></label>
      </div>
      <label className="grid gap-1.5 text-sm font-bold">Nome completo<input required minLength={3} maxLength={120} value={form.name} onChange={set("name")} placeholder="Seu nome" autoComplete="name" className={input} /></label>
      <label className="grid gap-1.5 text-sm font-bold">E-mail<input required type="email" maxLength={160} value={form.email} onChange={set("email")} placeholder="voce@exemplo.com" autoComplete="email" className={input} /></label>
      <label className="grid gap-1.5 text-sm font-bold">NÃºmero do RA<input required maxLength={30} value={form.ra} onChange={set("ra")} placeholder="202600000" className={input} /></label>
    </div>
    {error && <p className="mt-3 rounded-lg bg-destructive/10 p-3 text-xs font-bold text-destructive">{error}</p>}
    <Button size="lg" className="mt-4 w-full" type="submit" disabled={busy}>{busy ? "Enviando..." : "Realizar InscriÃ§Ã£o"}</Button>
    <p className="mt-3 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground"><ShieldCheck size={15} /> Gratuita e com confirmaÃ§Ã£o imediata</p>
  </form>;
}

function Info({ icon: Icon, text }: { icon: typeof CalendarDays; text: string }) { return <span className="flex items-center gap-2 font-semibold"><Icon size={19} className="text-gold" />{text}</span>; }


==================== src\routes\eventos.index.tsx ====================
import { createFileRoute, Link } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { Award, CalendarDays, ChevronRight, Images, MapPin } from "lucide-react";
import { useState } from "react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { type EventCategory, type EventRow, categoryLabel, eventCategories, formatEventDays, hasEnded, isRegistrationOpen, usePublishedEvents } from "@/lib/data";
import { pageText, usePageContent } from "@/lib/pages";
import hero from "@/assets/semana-juridica.jpg";

export const Route = createFileRoute("/eventos/")({ head: () => ({ meta: [
  { title: "Eventos â€” Sistema Themis" }, { name: "description", content: "Eventos do DiretÃ³rio AcadÃªmico de Direito com inscriÃ§Ã£o gratuita." }, { property: "og:title", content: "Eventos â€” Sistema Themis" }, { property: "og:description", content: "Palestras, debates e encontros do DA de Direito." }, { property: "og:type", content: "website" }, { name: "twitter:card", content: "summary_large_image" },
] }), component: EventsPage });

function EventsPage() {
  const { data: events, isLoading } = usePublishedEvents();
  const { data: pages } = usePageContent();
  const [category, setCategory] = useState<EventCategory | "all">("all");
  const upcoming = events?.filter((e) => !hasEnded(e)) ?? [];
  const past = events?.filter(hasEnded).reverse() ?? [];
  const usedCategories = eventCategories.filter((c) => upcoming.some((e) => e.category === c.id));
  const visible = category === "all" ? upcoming : upcoming.filter((e) => e.category === category);

  return <div className="app-container py-8 sm:py-12">
    <p className="text-sm font-bold uppercase text-gold">{pageText(pages, "events_label")}</p><h1 className="mt-1 text-3xl font-bold">{pageText(pages, "events_title")}</h1>
    <p className="mt-3 max-w-2xl text-muted-foreground">{pageText(pages, "events_text")}</p>
    {usedCategories.length > 1 && <div className="-mx-4 mt-6 flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto px-4 sm:mx-0 sm:px-0" role="tablist" aria-label="Categorias">
      {[{ id: "all" as const, label: "Todos" }, ...usedCategories].map((c) => <button key={c.id} role="tab" aria-selected={category === c.id} onClick={() => setCategory(c.id)} className={`h-10 shrink-0 rounded-full border px-4 text-sm font-bold ${category === c.id ? "border-primary bg-primary text-primary-foreground" : "border-border bg-card text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"}`}>{c.label}</button>)}
    </div>}
    <div className="mt-6 grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
      {isLoading && [0, 1, 2].map((i) => <div key={i} className="h-72 animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-muted" />)}
      {visible.map((e) => <EventCard key={e.id} event={e} />)}
    </div>
    {!isLoading && visible.length === 0 && <p className="rounded-lg border border-border bg-card p-6 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">{pageText(pages, "events_empty")}</p>}

    <section className="mt-10 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4 rounded-xl border border-gold/40 bg-card p-5">
      <span className="grid size-12 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-lg bg-primary text-gold"><Award size={22} /></span>
      <div className="min-w-0 flex-1"><h2 className="font-bold">{pageText(pages, "events_certificate_title")}</h2><p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{pageText(pages, "events_certificate_text")}</p></div>
      <Button asChild variant="outline"><Link to="/certificados">Emitir certificado</Link></Button>
    </section>

    {past.length > 0 && <>
      <h2 className="mt-12 text-xl font-bold">{pageText(pages, "events_past_title")}</h2>
      <div className="mt-4 grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">{past.map((e) => <PastEventCard key={e.id} event={e} />)}</div>
    </>}
  </div>;
}

function EventCard({ event }: { event: EventRow }) {
  const open = isRegistrationOpen(event);
  const { data: pages } = usePageContent();
  const image = event.image_url || pageText(pages, "fallback_image") || hero;
  return <Link to="/eventos/$slug" params={{ slug: event.slug }} className="group overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-border bg-card shadow-sm transition hover:border-primary">
    <img src={image} alt="" loading="lazy" width={800} height={450} className="aspect-[16/9] w-full object-cover grayscale" />
    <div className="p-4">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
        <span className={`inline-flex rounded-full px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-bold uppercase ${open ? "bg-success-soft text-success" : "bg-muted text-muted-foreground"}`}>{open ? "InscriÃ§Ãµes abertas" : "InscriÃ§Ãµes encerradas"}</span>
        <span className="inline-flex rounded-full bg-navy-soft px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-bold uppercase text-primary">{categoryLabel(event.category)}</span>
      </div>
      <h3 className="mt-2 text-lg font-bold leading-snug">{event.title}</h3>
      <div className="mt-3 space-y-1.5 text-sm text-muted-foreground"><p className="flex items-center gap-2"><CalendarDays size={16} className="text-gold" />{formatEventDays(event)}</p>{event.location && <p className="flex items-center gap-2"><MapPin size={16} className="text-gold" />{event.location}</p>}</div>
      <p className="mt-4 flex items-center gap-1 text-sm font-bold text-primary">Ver detalhes <ChevronRight size={16} className="transition group-hover:translate-x-0.5" /></p>
    </div>
  </Link>;
}

function PastEventCard({ event }: { event: EventRow }) {
  const { data: pages } = usePageContent();
  return <article className="flex items-center gap-4 rounded-xl border border-border bg-card p-3">
    <img src={event.image_url || pageText(pages, "fallback_image") || hero} alt="" loading="lazy" width={160} height={160} className="size-16 shrink-0 rounded-lg object-cover opacity-80 grayscale" />
    <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
      <h3 className="truncate font-bold">{event.title}</h3>
      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{formatEventDays(event)} Â· {categoryLabel(event.category)}</p>
      <div className="mt-1 flex flex-wrap gap-x-3 gap-y-1">
        <Link to="/eventos/$slug" params={{ slug: event.slug }} search={{ aba: "fotos" }} className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-bold text-primary"><Images size={13} /> Ver fotos</Link>
        {event.certificate_enabled && <Link to="/certificados" search={{ evento: event.slug }} className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-bold text-primary"><Award size={13} /> Emitir certificado</Link>}
      </div>
    </div>
  </article>;
}


==================== src\routes\index.tsx ====================
import { createFileRoute } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { Link } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { ArrowRight, Bell, CalendarDays, MapPin, UserRound } from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { ProductCard, ProductCardSkeleton } from "@/components/product-card";
import { formatEventDays, groupBySector, isRegistrationOpen, pickHighlight, useActiveProducts, useAnnouncements, useBoardMembers, usePublishedEvents } from "@/lib/data";
import { pageText, usePageContent } from "@/lib/pages";
import hero from "@/assets/semana-juridica.jpg";

export const Route = createFileRoute("/")({
  head: () => ({ meta: [
    { title: "Sistema Themis â€” Portal do Direito" },
    { name: "description", content: "Eventos, produtos e avisos do DiretÃ³rio AcadÃªmico de Direito." },
    { property: "og:title", content: "Sistema Themis â€” Portal do Direito" },
    { property: "og:description", content: "Eventos, produtos e avisos do DiretÃ³rio AcadÃªmico de Direito." },
    { property: "og:type", content: "website" }, { name: "twitter:card", content: "summary_large_image" },
  ] }),
  component: Index,
});

function Index() {
  const { data: events = [] } = usePublishedEvents();
  const { data: products, isLoading: loadingProducts } = useActiveProducts();
  const { data: announcements = [] } = useAnnouncements();
  const { data: members = [] } = useBoardMembers();
  const { data: pages } = usePageContent();
  const highlight = pickHighlight(events);
  const banner = pageText(pages, "fallback_image") || hero;
  return <>
    <section className="relative min-h-[500px] overflow-hidden bg-primary text-primary-foreground md:min-h-[560px]">
      <img src={highlight?.image_url || banner} alt={highlight?.title ?? pageText(pages, "home_title")} width={1600} height={912} className="absolute inset-0 h-full w-full object-cover object-[62%_center] opacity-70 grayscale" />
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-r from-primary via-primary/90 to-primary/10" />
      <div className="app-container relative flex min-h-[500px] items-end pb-10 pt-24 md:min-h-[560px] md:items-center md:py-20">
        {highlight ? <div className="max-w-xl">
          <span className="inline-flex rounded-full bg-gold px-3 py-1 text-xs font-extrabold uppercase text-gold-foreground">{isRegistrationOpen(highlight) ? "InscriÃ§Ãµes abertas" : "PrÃ³ximo evento"}</span>
          <h1 className="mt-4 text-4xl font-bold leading-tight sm:text-5xl">{highlight.title}</h1>
          {highlight.summary && <p className="mt-4 max-w-lg text-base leading-relaxed text-primary-foreground/85 sm:text-lg">{highlight.summary}</p>}
          <div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap gap-x-5 gap-y-2 text-sm font-semibold"><span className="flex items-center gap-2"><CalendarDays size={18} className="text-gold" /> {formatEventDays(highlight)}</span>{highlight.location && <span className="flex items-center gap-2"><MapPin size={18} className="text-gold" /> {highlight.location}</span>}</div>
          <Button asChild variant="gold" size="lg" className="mt-7 w-full sm:w-auto"><Link to="/eventos/$slug" params={{ slug: highlight.slug }}>Ver evento <ArrowRight /></Link></Button>
        </div> : <div className="max-w-xl">
          <span className="inline-flex rounded-full bg-gold px-3 py-1 text-xs font-extrabold uppercase text-gold-foreground">{pageText(pages, "home_badge")}</span>
          <h1 className="mt-4 text-4xl font-bold leading-tight sm:text-5xl">{pageText(pages, "home_title")}</h1>
          <p className="mt-4 max-w-lg text-base leading-relaxed text-primary-foreground/85 sm:text-lg">{pageText(pages, "home_text")}</p>
          <Button asChild variant="gold" size="lg" className="mt-7 w-full sm:w-auto"><Link to="/eventos">Ver eventos <ArrowRight /></Link></Button>
        </div>}
      </div>
    </section>
    <section className="app-container py-9"><div className="flex items-end justify-between"><div><p className="text-sm font-bold uppercase text-gold">{pageText(pages, "home_store_label")}</p><h2 className="mt-1 text-2xl font-bold">{pageText(pages, "home_store_title")}</h2></div><Link to="/produtos" className="text-sm font-bold text-primary">Ver todos</Link></div>
      <div className="-mx-4 mt-5 flex snap-x gap-4 overflow-x-auto px-4 pb-3 sm:mx-0 sm:grid sm:grid-cols-3 sm:px-0">
        {loadingProducts && [0, 1, 2].map((i) => <div key={i} className="w-[78vw] max-w-[290px] shrink-0 sm:w-auto sm:max-w-none"><ProductCardSkeleton /></div>)}
        {products?.slice(0, 6).map((product) => <div key={product.id} className="w-[78vw] max-w-[290px] shrink-0 snap-start sm:w-auto sm:max-w-none"><ProductCard product={product} /></div>)}
        {products?.length === 0 && <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Nenhum produto disponÃ­vel no momento.</p>}
      </div>
    </section>
    {members.length > 0 && <section className="border-y border-border bg-card">
      <div className="app-container py-9">
        <div className="text-center"><p className="text-sm font-bold uppercase text-gold">{pageText(pages, "home_members_label")}</p><h2 className="mt-1 text-2xl font-bold">{pageText(pages, "home_members_title")}</h2><p className="mx-auto mt-2 max-w-xl text-sm text-muted-foreground">{pageText(pages, "home_members_text")}</p></div>
        <MemberGroup title="Chapa" members={members.filter((member) => member.in_chapa)} />
        <MemberGroup title="Demais integrantes" members={members.filter((member) => !member.in_chapa)} />
      </div>
    </section>}
    {announcements.length > 0 && <section className="app-container py-9"><div className="mx-auto max-w-2xl space-y-8 text-center">
      {announcements.map((a) => <div key={a.id}><span className="mx-auto grid size-11 place-items-center rounded-full bg-navy-soft text-primary"><Bell size={21} /></span><p className="mt-3 text-sm font-bold uppercase text-gold">{pageText(pages, "home_notice_label")}</p><h2 className="mt-1 text-xl font-bold">{a.title}</h2>{a.body && <p className="mt-2 whitespace-pre-line text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">{a.body}</p>}</div>)}
    </div></section>}
  </>;
}

function MemberGroup({ title, members }: { title: string; members: { id: string; name: string; role: string; photo_url: string | null; sector: string; instagram: string | null }[] }) {
  if (members.length === 0) return null;
  const sectors = groupBySector(members);
  return <div className="mt-8">
    <h3 className="text-center text-sm font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-gold">{title}</h3>
    {sectors.map(({ sector, items }) => <div key={sector} className="mt-5">
      {sectors.length > 1 && <h4 className="mb-3 text-center text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-muted-foreground">{sector}</h4>}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3 sm:gap-5 lg:grid-cols-6">
        {items.map((member) => <article key={member.id} className="group overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-border bg-background transition-colors duration-300 hover:border-gold/50">
          <div className="overflow-hidden">
            {member.photo_url ? <img src={member.photo_url} alt={`Foto de ${member.name}`} loading="lazy" width={768} height={768} className="aspect-square w-full object-cover transition duration-500 ease-out motion-safe:group-hover:scale-110" /> : <span className="grid aspect-square w-full place-items-center bg-muted text-muted-foreground transition duration-500 ease-out motion-safe:group-hover:scale-110"><UserRound size={40} /></span>}
          </div>
          <div className="p-3 text-center"><h4 className="text-sm font-bold leading-snug">{member.name}</h4><p className="mt-0.5 text-[11px] font-bold uppercase leading-snug tracking-wide text-gold">{member.role}</p>
            {member.instagram && <a href={`https://instagram.com/${member.instagram}`} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" className="mt-1 block truncate text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground hover:text-primary">@{member.instagram}</a>}
          </div>
        </article>)}
      </div>
    </div>)}
  </div>;
}

==================== src\routes\reset-password.tsx ====================
import { createFileRoute, useNavigate } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { useState } from "react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";

export const Route = createFileRoute("/reset-password")({
  head: () => ({ meta: [
    { title: "Nova senha â€” Sistema Themis" }, { name: "description", content: "Defina uma nova senha para sua conta." },
    { property: "og:title", content: "Nova senha â€” Sistema Themis" }, { property: "og:description", content: "RedefiniÃ§Ã£o de senha do portal." },
    { property: "og:type", content: "website" }, { name: "twitter:card", content: "summary_large_image" }, { name: "robots", content: "noindex" },
  ] }),
  component: ResetPage,
});

function ResetPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  return <div className="app-container grid min-h-[70vh] place-items-center py-10">
    <form className="w-full max-w-md rounded-xl border border-border bg-card p-6 shadow-sm" onSubmit={async (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      const { error } = await supabase.auth.updateUser({ password });
      if (error) setError("NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel salvar. Se veio por e-mail, o link pode ter expirado: solicite outro."); else navigate({ to: "/admin" });
    }}>
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Definir nova senha</h1>
      <label className="mt-6 grid gap-1.5 text-sm font-bold">Nova senha<input required minLength={8} type="password" autoComplete="new-password" value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} className="h-12 rounded-lg border border-input bg-background px-3 font-normal outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-ring" /></label>
      {error && <p className="mt-3 rounded-lg bg-destructive/10 p-3 text-xs font-bold text-destructive">{error}</p>}
      <Button size="lg" className="mt-5 w-full" type="submit">Salvar senha</Button>
    </form>
  </div>;
}

import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import {
  Outlet,
  Link,
  createRootRouteWithContext,
  useRouter,
  HeadContent,
  Scripts,
  type ErrorComponentProps,
} from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { useEffect, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { AppShell } from "../components/app-shell";
import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
import { fetchPageContent, pageDefaults, type PageContentPayload } from "@/lib/pages";

import appCss from "../styles.css?url";

function NotFoundComponent() {
  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-background px-4">
      <div className="max-w-md text-center">
        <h1 className="text-7xl font-bold text-foreground">404</h1>
        <h2 className="mt-4 text-xl font-semibold text-foreground">Page not found</h2>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
          The page you're looking for doesn't exist or has been moved.
        </p>
        <div className="mt-6">
          <Link
            to="/"
            className="inline-flex items-center justify-center rounded-md bg-primary px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground transition-colors hover:bg-primary/90"
          >
            Go home
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function ErrorComponent({ error, reset }: ErrorComponentProps) {
  console.error(error);
  const router = useRouter();

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-background px-4">
      <div className="max-w-md text-center">
        <h1 className="text-xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-foreground">
          This page didn't load
        </h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
          Something went wrong on our end. You can try refreshing or head back home.
        </p>
        <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-2">
          <button
            onClick={() => {
              router.invalidate();
              reset();
            }}
            className="inline-flex items-center justify-center rounded-md bg-primary px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground transition-colors hover:bg-primary/90"
          >
            Try again
          </button>
          <a
            href="/"
            className="inline-flex items-center justify-center rounded-md border border-input bg-background px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-foreground transition-colors hover:bg-accent"
          >
            Go home
          </a>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export const Route = createRootRouteWithContext<{ queryClient: QueryClient }>()({
  loader: async (): Promise<PageContentPayload> => {
    try {
      return { content: await fetchPageContent(), fetchedAt: Date.now() };
    } catch {
      return { content: pageDefaults, fetchedAt: 0 };
    }
  },
  head: ({ loaderData }) => {
    const logo = loaderData?.content.site_logo.trim();
    return {
    meta: [
      { charSet: "utf-8" },
      { name: "viewport", content: "width=device-width, initial-scale=1" },
      { title: "Sistema Themis" },
      { name: "description", content: "Portal oficial do DiretÃ³rio AcadÃªmico de Direito." },
      { name: "author", content: "DiretÃ³rio AcadÃªmico de Direito" },
      { property: "og:type", content: "website" },
      { name: "twitter:card", content: "summary_large_image" },
    ],
    links: [
      {
        rel: "stylesheet",
        href: appCss,
      },
      { rel: "preconnect", href: "https://fonts.googleapis.com" },
      { rel: "preconnect", href: "https://fonts.gstatic.com", crossOrigin: "anonymous" },
      { rel: "stylesheet", href: "https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Manrope:wght@400;500;600;700;800&family=Sora:wght@600;700&display=swap" },
      logo ? { rel: "icon", href: logo } : { rel: "icon", href: "/favicon.svg", type: "image/svg+xml" },
    ],
  };
  },
  shellComponent: RootShell,
  component: RootComponent,
  notFoundComponent: NotFoundComponent,
  errorComponent: ErrorComponent,
});

function RootShell({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <html lang="pt-BR">
      <head>
        <HeadContent />
      </head>
      <body>
        {children}
        <Scripts />
      </body>
    </html>
  );
}

function RootComponent() {
  const { queryClient } = Route.useRouteContext();
  const router = useRouter();
  useEffect(() => {
    const { data } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((event) => {
      if (event !== "SIGNED_IN" && event !== "SIGNED_OUT" && event !== "USER_UPDATED") return;
      router.invalidate();
      if (event !== "SIGNED_OUT") queryClient.invalidateQueries();
    });
    return () => data.subscription.unsubscribe();
  }, [router, queryClient]);

  return (
    <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
      <AppShell><Outlet /></AppShell>
    </QueryClientProvider>
  );
}

==================== src\router.tsx ====================
import { QueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { createRouter } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { routeTree } from "./routeTree.gen";

export const getRouter = () => {
  const queryClient = new QueryClient();

  const router = createRouter({
    routeTree,
    context: { queryClient },
    scrollRestoration: true,
    defaultPreloadStaleTime: 0,
  });

  return router;
};

==================== src\server.ts ====================
import "./lib/error-capture";

import { consumeLastCapturedError } from "./lib/error-capture";
import { renderErrorPage } from "./lib/error-page";

type ServerEntry = {
  fetch: (request: Request, env: unknown, ctx: unknown) => Promise<Response> | Response;
};

let serverEntryPromise: Promise<ServerEntry> | undefined;

async function getServerEntry(): Promise<ServerEntry> {
  if (!serverEntryPromise) {
    serverEntryPromise = import("@tanstack/react-start/server-entry").then(
      (m) => (m.default ?? m) as ServerEntry,
    );
  }
  return serverEntryPromise;
}

// h3 swallows in-handler throws into a normal 500 Response with body
// {"unhandled":true,"message":"HTTPError"} â€” try/catch alone never fires for those.
async function normalizeCatastrophicSsrResponse(response: Response): Promise<Response> {
  if (response.status < 500) return response;
  const contentType = response.headers.get("content-type") ?? "";
  if (!contentType.includes("application/json")) return response;

  const body = await response.clone().text();
  if (!isH3SwallowedErrorBody(body)) return response;

  console.error(consumeLastCapturedError() ?? new Error(`h3 swallowed SSR error: ${body}`));
  return new Response(renderErrorPage(), {
    status: 500,
    headers: { "content-type": "text/html; charset=utf-8" },
  });
}

function isH3SwallowedErrorBody(body: string): boolean {
  try {
    const payload = JSON.parse(body) as { unhandled?: unknown; message?: unknown };
    return payload.unhandled === true && payload.message === "HTTPError";
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

export default {
  async fetch(request: Request, env: unknown, ctx: unknown) {
    try {
      const handler = await getServerEntry();
      const response = await handler.fetch(request, env, ctx);
      return await normalizeCatastrophicSsrResponse(response);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);
      return new Response(renderErrorPage(), {
        status: 500,
        headers: { "content-type": "text/html; charset=utf-8" },
      });
    }
  },
};


==================== src\start.ts ====================
import { createStart, createCsrfMiddleware, createMiddleware } from "@tanstack/react-start";

import { renderErrorPage } from "./lib/error-page";
import { attachSupabaseAuth } from "@/integrations/supabase/auth-attacher";

const errorMiddleware = createMiddleware().server(async ({ next }) => {
  try {
    return await next();
  } catch (error) {
    if (error != null && typeof error === "object" && "statusCode" in error) {
      throw error;
    }
    console.error(error);
    return new Response(renderErrorPage(), {
      status: 500,
      headers: { "content-type": "text/html; charset=utf-8" },
    });
  }
});

// Start installs this automatically when src/start.ts is absent; defining the
// file opts out, so re-add it explicitly to keep server functions protected
// from cross-site requests.
const csrfMiddleware = createCsrfMiddleware({
  filter: (ctx) => ctx.handlerType === "serverFn",
});

export const startInstance = createStart(() => ({
  functionMiddleware: [attachSupabaseAuth],
  requestMiddleware: [errorMiddleware, csrfMiddleware],
}));


==================== supabase\functions\admin-users\index.ts ====================
import "jsr:@supabase/functions-js/edge-runtime.d.ts";
import { createClient } from "npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2";

const cors = {
  "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",
  "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type",
  "Access-Control-Allow-Methods": "POST, OPTIONS",
};

const json = (body: unknown, status = 200) =>
  new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status, headers: { ...cors, "Content-Type": "application/json" } });

const admin = createClient(Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL")!, Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY")!, {
  auth: { persistSession: false, autoRefreshToken: false },
});

async function logAccess(actorId: string, actorEmail: string | undefined, action: "criou" | "editou" | "excluiu", entityId: string, summary: string) {
  await admin.from("activity_log").insert({
    actor_id: actorId,
    actor_email: actorEmail ?? null,
    action,
    area: "acesso",
    entity_id: entityId,
    summary: summary.slice(0, 300),
  });
}

async function hasRole(userId: string, role: "owner" | "admin") {
  const { data } = await admin.from("user_roles").select("id").eq("user_id", userId).eq("role", role).maybeSingle();
  return !!data;
}

const validEmail = (v: unknown): v is string => typeof v === "string" && /^[^@\s]+@[^@\s]+\.[^@\s]+$/.test(v) && v.length <= 160;
const validPassword = (v: unknown): v is string => typeof v === "string" && v.length >= 8 && v.length <= 72;

Deno.serve(async (req) => {
  if (req.method === "OPTIONS") return new Response("ok", { headers: cors });
  if (req.method !== "POST") return json({ error: "method_not_allowed" }, 405);

  const token = req.headers.get("Authorization")?.replace(/^Bearer\s+/i, "");
  if (!token) return json({ error: "unauthorized" }, 401);
  const { data: caller, error: callerError } = await admin.auth.getUser(token);
  if (callerError || !caller.user) return json({ error: "unauthorized" }, 401);
  if (!(await hasRole(caller.user.id, "owner"))) return json({ error: "forbidden" }, 403);

  let body: Record<string, unknown>;
  try { body = await req.json(); } catch { return json({ error: "invalid_body" }, 400); }

  if (body.action === "create") {
    const email = typeof body.email === "string" ? body.email.trim().toLowerCase() : "";
    if (!validEmail(email)) return json({ error: "invalid_email" }, 400);
    if (!validPassword(body.password)) return json({ error: "weak_password" }, 400);
    const { data, error } = await admin.auth.admin.createUser({ email, password: body.password, email_confirm: true });
    if (error || !data.user) return json({ error: error?.code === "email_exists" ? "email_exists" : "create_failed" }, 400);
    const { error: roleError } = await admin.from("user_roles").insert({ user_id: data.user.id, role: "admin" });
    if (roleError) {
      await admin.auth.admin.deleteUser(data.user.id);
      return json({ error: "create_failed" }, 500);
    }
    await logAccess(caller.user.id, caller.user.email, "criou", data.user.id, `criou o acesso de ${email}`);
    return json({ ok: true, user_id: data.user.id });
  }

  if (body.action === "delete" || body.action === "set_password") {
    const target = typeof body.user_id === "string" ? body.user_id : "";
    if (!target) return json({ error: "invalid_user" }, 400);
    if (target === caller.user.id || (await hasRole(target, "owner"))) return json({ error: "protected_user" }, 400);
    if (!(await hasRole(target, "admin"))) return json({ error: "invalid_user" }, 400);

    const { data: targetUser } = await admin.auth.admin.getUserById(target);
    const targetEmail = targetUser.user?.email ?? "conta removida";
    if (body.action === "delete") {
      const { error } = await admin.auth.admin.deleteUser(target);
      if (error) return json({ error: "delete_failed" }, 500);
      await logAccess(caller.user.id, caller.user.email, "excluiu", target, `removeu o acesso de ${targetEmail}`);
      return json({ ok: true });
    }
    if (!validPassword(body.password)) return json({ error: "weak_password" }, 400);
    const { error } = await admin.auth.admin.updateUserById(target, { password: body.password });
    if (error) return json({ error: "update_failed" }, 500);
    await logAccess(caller.user.id, caller.user.email, "editou", target, `alterou a senha do acesso de ${targetEmail}`);
    return json({ ok: true });
  }

  if (body.action === "set_email") {
    const target = typeof body.user_id === "string" ? body.user_id : "";
    const email = typeof body.email === "string" ? body.email.trim().toLowerCase() : "";
    if (!target) return json({ error: "invalid_user" }, 400);
    if (!validEmail(email)) return json({ error: "invalid_email" }, 400);
    if (target === caller.user.id || (await hasRole(target, "owner"))) return json({ error: "protected_user" }, 400);
    if (!(await hasRole(target, "admin"))) return json({ error: "invalid_user" }, 400);
    const { data: targetUser } = await admin.auth.admin.getUserById(target);
    const previous = targetUser.user?.email ?? "conta";
    const { error } = await admin.auth.admin.updateUserById(target, { email, email_confirm: true });
    if (error) return json({ error: error.code === "email_exists" ? "email_exists" : "update_failed" }, 400);
    await logAccess(caller.user.id, caller.user.email, "editou", target, `alterou o e-mail do acesso de ${previous} para ${email}`);
    return json({ ok: true });
  }

  return json({ error: "unknown_action" }, 400);
});


==================== supabase\functions\event-emails\index.ts ====================
import "jsr:@supabase/functions-js/edge-runtime.d.ts";
import { createClient } from "npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2";

// Public on purpose: both actions only e-mail registrants once (tracked by *_sent_at),
// so calling them again cannot spam anyone.

const cors = {
  "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",
  "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type",
  "Access-Control-Allow-Methods": "POST, OPTIONS",
};

const json = (body: unknown, status = 200) =>
  new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status, headers: { ...cors, "Content-Type": "application/json" } });

const db = createClient(Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL")!, Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY")!, {
  auth: { persistSession: false, autoRefreshToken: false },
});

const RESEND_API_KEY = Deno.env.get("RESEND_API_KEY");
const EMAIL_FROM = Deno.env.get("EMAIL_FROM") ?? "DA de Direito <onboarding@resend.dev>";
const SITE_URL = (Deno.env.get("SITE_URL") ?? "http://localhost:8080").replace(/\/$/, "");
const TZ = "America/Sao_Paulo";

type EventInfo = { id: string; slug: string; title: string; location: string | null; starts_at: string; ends_at: string | null };
type Registration = { id: string; full_name: string; email: string; events: EventInfo };

const escape = (s: string) => s.replace(/[&<>"']/g, (c) => ({ "&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", '"': "&quot;", "'": "&#39;" })[c]!);
const day = (iso: string) => new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", { timeZone: TZ, day: "numeric", month: "long" }).format(new Date(iso));
const hour = (iso: string) => new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", { timeZone: TZ, hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" }).format(new Date(iso));

function when(ev: EventInfo) {
  const start = `${day(ev.starts_at)}, Ã s ${hour(ev.starts_at)}`;
  return ev.ends_at && day(ev.ends_at) !== day(ev.starts_at) ? `${start} atÃ© ${day(ev.ends_at)}` : start;
}

function layout(title: string, intro: string, ev: EventInfo) {
  const url = `${SITE_URL}/eventos/${ev.slug}`;
  return `<!doctype html><html><body style="margin:0;background:#f4f4f4;font-family:Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif;color:#1c1c1c">
<table role="presentation" width="100%" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" style="padding:24px 0"><tr><td align="center">
<table role="presentation" width="560" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" style="max-width:560px;background:#ffffff;border-radius:12px;overflow:hidden">
<tr><td style="background:#1c1c1c;padding:20px 28px;color:#b8963c;font-weight:bold;letter-spacing:1px;font-size:13px">DIRETÃ“RIO ACADÃŠMICO DE DIREITO</td></tr>
<tr><td style="padding:28px">
<h1 style="margin:0 0 12px;font-size:22px">${escape(title)}</h1>
<p style="margin:0 0 20px;line-height:1.6;color:#444">${intro}</p>
<table role="presentation" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" style="width:100%;border:1px solid #e5e5e5;border-radius:8px"><tr><td style="padding:16px">
<p style="margin:0;font-weight:bold;font-size:16px">${escape(ev.title)}</p>
<p style="margin:6px 0 0;color:#444">${escape(when(ev))}</p>
${ev.location ? `<p style="margin:4px 0 0;color:#444">${escape(ev.location)}</p>` : ""}
</td></tr></table>
<p style="margin:24px 0 0"><a href="${url}" style="display:inline-block;background:#1c1c1c;color:#ffffff;text-decoration:none;font-weight:bold;padding:12px 20px;border-radius:8px">Ver evento</a></p>
<p style="margin:24px 0 0;font-size:12px;color:#888">A presenÃ§a Ã© marcada pela organizaÃ§Ã£o na entrada. Quem tiver presenÃ§a confirmada pode emitir o certificado no site, quando liberado.</p>
</td></tr></table></td></tr></table></body></html>`;
}

async function send(to: string, subject: string, html: string) {
  const r = await fetch("https://api.resend.com/emails", {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${RESEND_API_KEY}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ from: EMAIL_FROM, to: [to], subject, html }),
  });
  if (!r.ok) console.error("resend", r.status, await r.text());
  return r.ok;
}

const firstName = (name: string) => escape(name.trim().split(/\s+/)[0] ?? name);

async function sendConfirmation(eventId: string, email: string) {
  const { data } = await db.from("registrations")
    .select("id, full_name, email, events!inner(id, slug, title, location, starts_at, ends_at)")
    .eq("event_id", eventId).eq("email", email.trim().toLowerCase()).is("confirmation_sent_at", null).maybeSingle();
  const reg = data as Registration | null;
  if (!reg) return json({ sent: false, reason: "not_found" });
  const ok = await send(reg.email, `InscriÃ§Ã£o confirmada: ${reg.events.title}`,
    layout("InscriÃ§Ã£o confirmada!", `OlÃ¡, ${firstName(reg.full_name)}! Sua inscriÃ§Ã£o estÃ¡ garantida. Na vÃ©spera, mandamos um lembrete.`, reg.events));
  if (ok) await db.from("registrations").update({ confirmation_sent_at: new Date().toISOString() }).eq("id", reg.id);
  return json({ sent: ok });
}

async function sendReminders() {
  const now = new Date();
  const until = new Date(now.getTime() + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
  const { data, error } = await db.from("registrations")
    .select("id, full_name, email, events!inner(id, slug, title, location, starts_at, ends_at, published)")
    .is("reminder_sent_at", null)
    .eq("events.published", true)
    .gte("events.starts_at", now.toISOString())
    .lte("events.starts_at", until.toISOString())
    .limit(200);
  if (error) return json({ error: "query_failed" }, 500);
  let sent = 0;
  for (const reg of (data ?? []) as Registration[]) {
    const ok = await send(reg.email, `Lembrete: ${reg.events.title} Ã© amanhÃ£`,
      layout("Seu evento estÃ¡ chegando", `OlÃ¡, ${firstName(reg.full_name)}! Passando para lembrar do evento em que vocÃª se inscreveu.`, reg.events));
    if (ok) {
      sent++;
      await db.from("registrations").update({ reminder_sent_at: new Date().toISOString() }).eq("id", reg.id);
    }
  }
  return json({ sent, pending: (data?.length ?? 0) - sent });
}

Deno.serve(async (req) => {
  if (req.method === "OPTIONS") return new Response("ok", { headers: cors });
  if (req.method !== "POST") return json({ error: "method_not_allowed" }, 405);
  if (!RESEND_API_KEY) return json({ sent: false, reason: "not_configured" });

  let body: Record<string, unknown>;
  try { body = await req.json(); } catch { return json({ error: "invalid_body" }, 400); }

  if (body.type === "confirmation" && typeof body.event_id === "string" && typeof body.email === "string") {
    return sendConfirmation(body.event_id, body.email);
  }
  if (body.type === "reminders") return sendReminders();
  return json({ error: "unknown_type" }, 400);
});


==================== supabase\migrations\20260928225619_a2e70be0-9e64-433e-b8a6-ed4eb9ece6ae.sql ====================
CREATE TYPE public.app_role AS ENUM ('admin', 'aluno');

CREATE TABLE public.user_roles (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  role public.app_role NOT NULL,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  UNIQUE (user_id, role)
);
GRANT SELECT ON public.user_roles TO authenticated;
GRANT ALL ON public.user_roles TO service_role;
ALTER TABLE public.user_roles ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.has_role(_user_id uuid, _role public.app_role)
RETURNS boolean LANGUAGE sql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public AS $$
  SELECT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.user_roles WHERE user_id = _user_id AND role = _role)
$$;

CREATE POLICY "Users read own roles" ON public.user_roles FOR SELECT TO authenticated
  USING (auth.uid() = user_id OR public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin'));

-- Every new account is a student
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.handle_new_user()
RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public AS $$
BEGIN
  INSERT INTO public.user_roles (user_id, role) VALUES (NEW.id, 'aluno') ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;
  RETURN NEW;
END; $$;
CREATE TRIGGER on_auth_user_created AFTER INSERT ON auth.users
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.handle_new_user();

-- Bootstrap: first signed-in user may claim admin only while no admin exists
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.claim_first_admin()
RETURNS boolean LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public AS $$
BEGIN
  IF auth.uid() IS NULL THEN RETURN false; END IF;
  PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(424242);
  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.user_roles WHERE role = 'admin') THEN RETURN false; END IF;
  INSERT INTO public.user_roles (user_id, role) VALUES (auth.uid(), 'admin') ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;
  RETURN true;
END; $$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.admin_exists()
RETURNS boolean LANGUAGE sql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public AS $$
  SELECT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.user_roles WHERE role = 'admin')
$$;

-- Admins manage board membership by e-mail
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.list_board()
RETURNS TABLE (user_id uuid, email text, since timestamptz)
LANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public AS $$
BEGIN
  IF NOT public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin') THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'forbidden'; END IF;
  RETURN QUERY SELECT r.user_id, u.email::text, r.created_at
    FROM public.user_roles r JOIN auth.users u ON u.id = r.user_id
    WHERE r.role = 'admin' ORDER BY r.created_at;
END; $$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.set_board_member(_email text, _grant boolean)
RETURNS text LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public AS $$
DECLARE target uuid;
BEGIN
  IF NOT public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin') THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'forbidden'; END IF;
  SELECT id INTO target FROM auth.users WHERE lower(email) = lower(trim(_email));
  IF target IS NULL THEN RETURN 'not_found'; END IF;
  IF _grant THEN
    INSERT INTO public.user_roles (user_id, role) VALUES (target, 'admin') ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;
  ELSE
    IF target = auth.uid() THEN RETURN 'self'; END IF;
    DELETE FROM public.user_roles WHERE user_id = target AND role = 'admin';
  END IF;
  RETURN 'ok';
END; $$;

REVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.claim_first_admin(), public.admin_exists(), public.list_board(), public.set_board_member(text, boolean) FROM anon, public;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.claim_first_admin(), public.admin_exists(), public.list_board(), public.set_board_member(text, boolean) TO authenticated;

-- Shared updated_at trigger
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.set_updated_at()
RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql SET search_path = public AS $$
BEGIN
  NEW.updated_at = now();
  RETURN NEW;
END; $$;

-- â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ Events â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
CREATE TABLE public.events (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  slug text NOT NULL UNIQUE CHECK (slug ~ '^[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*$'),
  title text NOT NULL CHECK (length(trim(title)) > 0),
  summary text,
  description text,
  location text,
  starts_at timestamptz NOT NULL,
  ends_at timestamptz,
  image_url text,
  capacity integer CHECK (capacity IS NULL OR capacity > 0),
  registration_open boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
  published boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  featured boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  CHECK (ends_at IS NULL OR ends_at >= starts_at)
);
CREATE INDEX events_starts_at_idx ON public.events (starts_at);
CREATE TRIGGER events_updated_at BEFORE UPDATE ON public.events
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.set_updated_at();

CREATE TABLE public.event_schedule (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  event_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES public.events(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  label text NOT NULL CHECK (length(trim(label)) > 0),
  sort_order integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
);
CREATE INDEX event_schedule_event_idx ON public.event_schedule (event_id, sort_order);

-- Registrations are free and need no account; they are written only through register_for_event().
CREATE TABLE public.registrations (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  event_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES public.events(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  code text NOT NULL UNIQUE DEFAULT ('THM-EV-' || upper(substr(replace(gen_random_uuid()::text, '-', ''), 1, 8))),
  full_name text NOT NULL,
  email text NOT NULL,
  cpf text NOT NULL,
  phone text NOT NULL,
  ra text,
  user_id uuid REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  consent_at timestamptz NOT NULL,
  checked_in_at timestamptz,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX registrations_event_email_key ON public.registrations (event_id, lower(email));
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX registrations_event_cpf_key ON public.registrations (event_id, cpf);
CREATE INDEX registrations_email_idx ON public.registrations (lower(email));

);

);
CREATE TRIGGER products_updated_at BEFORE UPDATE ON public.products
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.set_updated_at();

-- â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ Sales (skeleton: no checkout writes yet) â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
CREATE TYPE public.order_status AS ENUM (
  'aguardando_pagamento', 'pago', 'em_producao', 'aguardando_retirada', 'entregue', 'cancelado'
);
CREATE TYPE public.payment_method AS ENUM ('pix', 'cartao');

CREATE TABLE public.orders (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  code text NOT NULL UNIQUE DEFAULT ('THM-' || upper(substr(replace(gen_random_uuid()::text, '-', ''), 1, 8))),
  user_id uuid REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  full_name text NOT NULL,
  email text NOT NULL,
  cpf text NOT NULL,
  phone text NOT NULL,
  ra text,
  status public.order_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'aguardando_pagamento',
  payment_method public.payment_method,
  subtotal numeric(10, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (subtotal >= 0),
  fee numeric(10, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (fee >= 0),
  total numeric(10, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (total >= 0),
  consent_at timestamptz NOT NULL,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
CREATE INDEX orders_user_idx ON public.orders (user_id);
CREATE INDEX orders_status_idx ON public.orders (status);
CREATE TRIGGER orders_updated_at BEFORE UPDATE ON public.orders
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.set_updated_at();

CREATE TABLE public.order_items (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  order_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES public.orders(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  product_id uuid REFERENCES public.products(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  product_name text NOT NULL,
  size text,
  quantity integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (quantity > 0),
  unit_price numeric(10, 2) NOT NULL CHECK (unit_price >= 0)
);
CREATE INDEX order_items_order_idx ON public.order_items (order_id);

-- â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ Row Level Security â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
ALTER TABLE public.events ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE public.event_schedule ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE public.registrations ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE public.announcements ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE public.products ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE public.orders ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE public.order_items ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE POLICY "Public reads published events" ON public.events FOR SELECT TO anon, authenticated USING (published);
CREATE POLICY "Admins manage events" ON public.events FOR ALL TO authenticated
  USING (public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin')) WITH CHECK (public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin'));

CREATE POLICY "Public reads schedule of published events" ON public.event_schedule FOR SELECT TO anon, authenticated
  USING (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.events e WHERE e.id = event_id AND e.published));
CREATE POLICY "Admins manage schedule" ON public.event_schedule FOR ALL TO authenticated
  USING (public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin')) WITH CHECK (public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin'));

CREATE POLICY "Admins manage registrations" ON public.registrations FOR ALL TO authenticated
  USING (public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin')) WITH CHECK (public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin'));

CREATE POLICY "Public reads published announcements" ON public.announcements FOR SELECT TO anon, authenticated USING (published);
CREATE POLICY "Admins manage announcements" ON public.announcements FOR ALL TO authenticated
  USING (public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin')) WITH CHECK (public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin'));

CREATE POLICY "Public reads active products" ON public.products FOR SELECT TO anon, authenticated USING (active);
CREATE POLICY "Admins manage products" ON public.products FOR ALL TO authenticated
  USING (public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin')) WITH CHECK (public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin'));

CREATE POLICY "Students read own orders" ON public.orders FOR SELECT TO authenticated USING (user_id = auth.uid());
CREATE POLICY "Admins manage orders" ON public.orders FOR ALL TO authenticated
  USING (public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin')) WITH CHECK (public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin'));

CREATE POLICY "Students read own order items" ON public.order_items FOR SELECT TO authenticated
  USING (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.orders o WHERE o.id = order_id AND o.user_id = auth.uid()));
CREATE POLICY "Admins manage order items" ON public.order_items FOR ALL TO authenticated
  USING (public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin')) WITH CHECK (public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin'));

GRANT SELECT ON public.events, public.event_schedule, public.announcements, public.products TO anon;
GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON public.events, public.event_schedule, public.announcements, public.products,
  public.registrations, public.orders, public.order_items TO authenticated;
GRANT ALL ON public.events, public.event_schedule, public.announcements, public.products,
  public.registrations, public.orders, public.order_items TO service_role;

-- â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ Registration functions â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.register_for_event(
  _event_id uuid, _full_name text, _email text, _cpf text, _phone text, _ra text, _consent boolean
)
RETURNS jsonb LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public AS $$
DECLARE
  ev public.events%ROWTYPE;
  taken integer;
  clean_cpf text := regexp_replace(coalesce(_cpf, ''), '\D', '', 'g');
  clean_phone text := regexp_replace(coalesce(_phone, ''), '\D', '', 'g');
  clean_email text := lower(trim(coalesce(_email, '')));
  new_code text;
BEGIN
  IF NOT coalesce(_consent, false) THEN RETURN jsonb_build_object('status', 'consent_required'); END IF;
  IF length(trim(coalesce(_full_name, ''))) < 3 OR length(_full_name) > 120
     OR clean_email !~ '^[^@\s]+@[^@\s]+\.[^@\s]+$' OR length(clean_email) > 160
     OR length(clean_cpf) <> 11 OR length(clean_phone) NOT BETWEEN 10 AND 11
     OR length(coalesce(_ra, '')) > 30 THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('status', 'invalid');
  END IF;

  SELECT * INTO ev FROM public.events WHERE id = _event_id AND published FOR UPDATE;
  IF NOT FOUND THEN RETURN jsonb_build_object('status', 'not_found'); END IF;
  IF NOT ev.registration_open OR coalesce(ev.ends_at, ev.starts_at) < now() THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('status', 'closed');
  END IF;

  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.registrations r WHERE r.event_id = ev.id AND (lower(r.email) = clean_email OR r.cpf = clean_cpf)) THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('status', 'duplicate');
  END IF;

  IF ev.capacity IS NOT NULL THEN
    SELECT count(*) INTO taken FROM public.registrations WHERE event_id = ev.id;
    IF taken >= ev.capacity THEN RETURN jsonb_build_object('status', 'full'); END IF;
  END IF;

  INSERT INTO public.registrations (event_id, full_name, email, cpf, phone, ra, user_id, consent_at)
  VALUES (ev.id, trim(_full_name), clean_email, clean_cpf, clean_phone, nullif(trim(coalesce(_ra, '')), ''), auth.uid(), now())
  RETURNING code INTO new_code;

  RETURN jsonb_build_object('status', 'ok', 'code', new_code);
END; $$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.event_spots_taken(_event_id uuid)
RETURNS integer LANGUAGE sql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public AS $$
  SELECT count(*)::integer FROM public.registrations r
  JOIN public.events e ON e.id = r.event_id
  WHERE r.event_id = _event_id AND e.published
$$;

-- Tickets of the signed-in student, matched by account or by the e-mail used in the form
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.my_registrations()
RETURNS TABLE (code text, created_at timestamptz, event_id uuid, event_slug text, event_title text,
               event_location text, event_starts_at timestamptz, event_ends_at timestamptz)
LANGUAGE sql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public AS $$
  SELECT r.code, r.created_at, e.id, e.slug, e.title, e.location, e.starts_at, e.ends_at
  FROM public.registrations r JOIN public.events e ON e.id = r.event_id
  WHERE auth.uid() IS NOT NULL
    AND (r.user_id = auth.uid() OR lower(r.email) = lower(coalesce(auth.jwt() ->> 'email', '')))
  ORDER BY e.starts_at DESC
$$;

REVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.register_for_event(uuid, text, text, text, text, text, boolean),
  public.event_spots_taken(uuid), public.my_registrations() FROM public;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.register_for_event(uuid, text, text, text, text, text, boolean),
  public.event_spots_taken(uuid) TO anon, authenticated;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.my_registrations() TO authenticated;
REVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.set_updated_at() FROM anon, authenticated, public;

==================== supabase\migrations\20261019040100_admin_only_accounts.sql ====================
-- Accounts are created only by the owner through the admin-users Edge Function; no self sign-up or self-promotion.
DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS on_auth_user_created ON auth.users;
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.handle_new_user();
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.claim_first_admin();
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.admin_exists();
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.set_board_member(text, boolean);
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.my_registrations();
DELETE FROM public.user_roles WHERE role = 'aluno';

DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.list_board();
CREATE FUNCTION public.list_board()
RETURNS TABLE (user_id uuid, email text, since timestamptz, is_owner boolean)
LANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public AS $$
BEGIN
  IF NOT public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin') THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'forbidden'; END IF;
  RETURN QUERY
    SELECT r.user_id, u.email::text, r.created_at,
           EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.user_roles o WHERE o.user_id = r.user_id AND o.role = 'owner')
    FROM public.user_roles r JOIN auth.users u ON u.id = r.user_id
    WHERE r.role = 'admin'
    ORDER BY 4 DESC, r.created_at;
END; $$;

REVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.list_board() FROM anon, public;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.list_board() TO authenticated;

RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
  ev public.events%ROWTYPE;
  clean_name text := trim(coalesce(_full_name, ''));
  clean_email text := lower(trim(coalesce(_email, '')));
  clean_ra text := trim(coalesce(_ra, ''));
  taken integer;
BEGIN
  IF length(clean_name) NOT BETWEEN 3 AND 120
     OR clean_email !~ '^[^@\s]+@[^@\s]+\.[^@\s]+$' OR length(clean_email) > 160
     OR length(clean_ra) NOT BETWEEN 1 AND 30 THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('status', 'invalid');
  END IF;

  SELECT * INTO ev FROM public.events WHERE id = _event_id AND published FOR UPDATE;
  IF NOT FOUND THEN RETURN jsonb_build_object('status', 'not_found'); END IF;
  IF NOT ev.registration_open OR coalesce(ev.ends_at, ev.starts_at) < now() THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('status', 'closed');
  END IF;

  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.registrations r WHERE r.event_id = ev.id AND (lower(r.email) = clean_email OR lower(r.ra) = lower(clean_ra))) THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('status', 'duplicate');

    SELECT count(*) INTO taken FROM public.registrations WHERE event_id = ev.id;
    IF taken >= ev.capacity THEN RETURN jsonb_build_object('status', 'full'); END IF;
  END IF;

  INSERT INTO public.registrations (event_id, full_name, email, ra) VALUES (ev.id, clean_name, clean_email, clean_ra);
  RETURN jsonb_build_object('status', 'ok');
END;
$$;

-- â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ Events: category, registration deadline, certificate â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

-- Registration with honeypot (_website must stay empty) and deadline check.
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.register_for_event(uuid, text, text, text);

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.register_for_event(
  _event_id uuid, _full_name text, _email text, _ra text, _website text DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
  ev public.events%ROWTYPE;
  clean_name text := trim(coalesce(_full_name, ''));
  clean_email text := lower(trim(coalesce(_email, '')));
  clean_ra text := trim(coalesce(_ra, ''));
  taken integer;
BEGIN
  -- Bots fill the hidden field; answer as if it worked so they don't retry.
  IF coalesce(_website, '') <> '' THEN RETURN jsonb_build_object('status', 'ok'); END IF;

  IF length(clean_name) NOT BETWEEN 3 AND 120
     OR clean_email !~ '^[^@\s]+@[^@\s]+\.[^@\s]+$' OR length(clean_email) > 160
     OR length(clean_ra) NOT BETWEEN 1 AND 30 THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('status', 'invalid');
  END IF;

  SELECT * INTO ev FROM public.events WHERE id = _event_id AND published FOR UPDATE;
  IF NOT FOUND THEN RETURN jsonb_build_object('status', 'not_found'); END IF;
  IF NOT ev.registration_open
     OR coalesce(ev.ends_at, ev.starts_at) < now()
     OR (ev.registration_closes_at IS NOT NULL AND ev.registration_closes_at <= now()) THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('status', 'closed');
  END IF;

  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.registrations r WHERE r.event_id = ev.id AND (lower(r.email) = clean_email OR lower(r.ra) = lower(clean_ra))) THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('status', 'duplicate');
  END IF;

  IF ev.capacity IS NOT NULL THEN
    SELECT count(*) INTO taken FROM public.registrations WHERE event_id = ev.id;
    IF taken >= ev.capacity THEN RETURN jsonb_build_object('status', 'full'); END IF;
  END IF;

  INSERT INTO public.registrations (event_id, full_name, email, ra) VALUES (ev.id, clean_name, clean_email, clean_ra);
  RETURN jsonb_build_object('status', 'ok');
END;
$$;

REVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.register_for_event(uuid, text, text, text, text) FROM public;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.register_for_event(uuid, text, text, text, text) TO anon, authenticated;

-- Certificate lookup: requires the e-mail AND the RA of a registration with confirmed attendance.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.issue_certificate(_event_id uuid, _email text, _ra text)
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
  ev public.events%ROWTYPE;
  reg public.registrations%ROWTYPE;
BEGIN
  SELECT * INTO ev FROM public.events
  WHERE id = _event_id AND published AND certificate_enabled AND coalesce(ends_at, starts_at) <= now();
  IF NOT FOUND THEN RETURN jsonb_build_object('status', 'unavailable'); END IF;

  SELECT * INTO reg FROM public.registrations r
  WHERE r.event_id = ev.id AND lower(r.email) = lower(trim(coalesce(_email, ''))) AND lower(r.ra) = lower(trim(coalesce(_ra, '')));
  IF NOT FOUND THEN RETURN jsonb_build_object('status', 'not_found'); END IF;
  IF reg.checked_in_at IS NULL THEN RETURN jsonb_build_object('status', 'no_attendance'); END IF;

  RETURN jsonb_build_object(
    'status', 'ok',
    'name', reg.full_name,
    'ra', reg.ra,
    'event_title', ev.title,
    'starts_at', ev.starts_at,
    'ends_at', ev.ends_at,
    'location', ev.location,
    'hours', ev.certificate_hours
  );
END;
$$;

REVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.issue_certificate(uuid, text, text) FROM public;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.issue_certificate(uuid, text, text) TO anon, authenticated;

ALTER TABLE public.board_members
  ADD COLUMN sector text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Diretoria' CHECK (length(trim(sector)) BETWEEN 2 AND 60),
  ADD COLUMN instagram text CHECK (instagram IS NULL OR instagram ~ '^[A-Za-z0-9._]{1,30}$');

  'event-email-reminders',
  '0 * * * *',
  $$
  SELECT net.http_post(
    url := 'https://qmjpkketagcczxshrmpk.supabase.co/functions/v1/event-emails',
    headers := '{"Content-Type": "application/json"}'::jsonb,

==================== supabase\migrations\20261115180000_board_member_logins.sql ====================
-- Each DA member can have their own panel login. Only the owner may set or clear that link.
ALTER TABLE public.board_members
  ADD COLUMN user_id uuid UNIQUE REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_board_member_login()
RETURNS trigger
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SET search_path = public
AS $$
BEGIN
  IF TG_OP = 'UPDATE' AND NEW.user_id IS NOT DISTINCT FROM OLD.user_id THEN
    RETURN NEW;
  END IF;
  IF TG_OP = 'INSERT' AND NEW.user_id IS NULL THEN
    RETURN NEW;
  END IF;
  IF auth.uid() IS NOT NULL AND NOT public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'owner') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'forbidden';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;

CREATE TRIGGER board_members_guard_login
  BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON public.board_members
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.guard_board_member_login();

-- Visitors do not receive the login link. Admins still read the full row.

GRANT SELECT (id, name, role, photo_url, sector, instagram, sort_order, active, created_at, updated_at)
  ON public.board_members TO anon;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.order_status_label(status public.order_status)
RETURNS text
LANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE AS $$
  SELECT CASE status
    WHEN 'aguardando_pagamento' THEN 'Aguardando pagamento'
    WHEN 'pago' THEN 'Pago'
    WHEN 'em_producao' THEN 'Em produÃ§Ã£o'
    WHEN 'aguardando_retirada' THEN 'Aguardando retirada'
    WHEN 'entregue' THEN 'Entregue'
    WHEN 'cancelado' THEN 'Cancelado'
  END;
$$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.audit_admin_change()
RETURNS trigger
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
  uid uuid := auth.uid();
  email text;
  act text;
  area text;
  entity uuid;
  summary text;
  label text;
  event_title text;
  fresh jsonb;
  previous jsonb;
BEGIN
  IF uid IS NULL OR auth.role() = 'service_role' OR auth.role() = 'anon' THEN
    IF TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN RETURN OLD; ELSE RETURN NEW; END IF;
  END IF;

  fresh := CASE WHEN TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN NULL ELSE to_jsonb(NEW) END;
  previous := CASE WHEN TG_OP = 'INSERT' THEN NULL ELSE to_jsonb(OLD) END;
  IF TG_OP = 'UPDATE' AND (fresh - 'updated_at' - 'sort_order') = (previous - 'updated_at' - 'sort_order') THEN
    RETURN NEW;
  END IF;

  SELECT u.email::text INTO email FROM auth.users u WHERE u.id = uid;
  entity := CASE WHEN TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN OLD.id ELSE NEW.id END;
  act := CASE TG_OP WHEN 'INSERT' THEN 'criou' WHEN 'DELETE' THEN 'excluiu' ELSE 'editou' END;

  IF TG_TABLE_NAME = 'events' THEN
    area := 'evento'; label := CASE WHEN TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN OLD.title ELSE NEW.title END;
    summary := act || ' o evento ' || label;
  ELSIF TG_TABLE_NAME = 'products' THEN
    area := 'produto'; label := CASE WHEN TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN OLD.name ELSE NEW.name END;
    summary := act || ' o produto ' || label;
  ELSIF TG_TABLE_NAME = 'announcements' THEN
    area := 'aviso'; label := CASE WHEN TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN OLD.title ELSE NEW.title END;
    summary := act || ' o aviso ' || label;
  ELSIF TG_TABLE_NAME = 'board_members' THEN
    area := 'membro'; label := CASE WHEN TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN OLD.name ELSE NEW.name END;
    IF TG_OP = 'UPDATE' AND NEW.user_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.user_id
       AND (fresh - 'updated_at' - 'sort_order' - 'user_id') = (previous - 'updated_at' - 'sort_order' - 'user_id') THEN
      summary := CASE WHEN NEW.user_id IS NULL THEN 'removeu o acesso de ' ELSE 'vinculou um acesso a ' END || label;
    ELSE
      summary := act || ' o membro ' || label;
    END IF;
  ELSIF TG_TABLE_NAME = 'orders' THEN
    area := 'pedido'; label := CASE WHEN TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN OLD.code ELSE NEW.code END;
    IF TG_OP = 'UPDATE' AND NEW.status IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.status THEN
      summary := 'alterou o pedido ' || label || ' para ' || public.order_status_label(NEW.status);
    ELSE
      summary := act || ' o pedido ' || label;
    END IF;
  ELSIF TG_TABLE_NAME = 'registrations' THEN
    area := 'inscricao';
    SELECT e.title INTO event_title FROM public.events e
      WHERE e.id = CASE WHEN TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN OLD.event_id ELSE NEW.event_id END;
    label := CASE WHEN TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN OLD.full_name ELSE NEW.full_name END;
    IF TG_OP = 'UPDATE' AND NEW.checked_in_at IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.checked_in_at THEN
      summary := CASE WHEN NEW.checked_in_at IS NULL THEN 'removeu a presenÃ§a de ' ELSE 'marcou a presenÃ§a de ' END
        || label || COALESCE(' em ' || event_title, '');
    ELSIF TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN
      summary := 'removeu a inscriÃ§Ã£o de ' || label || COALESCE(' em ' || event_title, '');
    ELSE
      summary := act || ' a inscriÃ§Ã£o de ' || label || COALESCE(' em ' || event_title, '');
    END IF;
  ELSE
    IF TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN RETURN OLD; ELSE RETURN NEW; END IF;
  END IF;

  INSERT INTO public.activity_log (actor_id, actor_email, action, area, entity_id, summary)
  VALUES (uid, email, act, area, entity, left(summary, 300));

  IF TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN RETURN OLD; ELSE RETURN NEW; END IF;
END;
$$;

REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.audit_admin_change() FROM public, anon;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.audit_admin_change() TO authenticated;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.order_status_label(public.order_status) FROM public, anon, authenticated;

CREATE TRIGGER events_audit AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE OR DELETE ON public.events
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.audit_admin_change();
CREATE TRIGGER products_audit AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE OR DELETE ON public.products
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.audit_admin_change();
CREATE TRIGGER announcements_audit AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE OR DELETE ON public.announcements
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.audit_admin_change();
CREATE TRIGGER board_members_audit AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE OR DELETE ON public.board_members
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.audit_admin_change();
CREATE TRIGGER orders_audit AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE OR DELETE ON public.orders
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.audit_admin_change();
CREATE TRIGGER registrations_audit AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE OR DELETE ON public.registrations
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.audit_admin_change();
